### [1] [Fine-Tuning Qwen3-27B for C-to-Rust Code Translation: A Three-Stage Curriculum of Pretraining, Debugging-Aware SFT, and Task-Specific SFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13681)
*Pu Zhao,Changdi Yang,Yixiao Chen,Yi Gao,Yifan Cao,Haochen Zeng,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向C到Rust安全迁移的三阶段微调方案，并结合SACTOR框架评估模型在语义保持、代码习惯性与自动修复方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在C到Rust转换中通常存在Rust惯用写法不足、跨语言语义等价性弱，以及难以利用编译器和运行时反馈进行修复等问题。研究旨在提升模型生成安全、可编译且符合Rust规范代码的能力。

Method: 以Qwen3-27B为基础，依次进行三阶段训练：持续预训练Rust相关语料以增强语法和标准库知识；基于Verus_Training_Data开展监督微调以学习调试和自修复；利用由LeetCode问题构造的C/Rust配对数据进行任务专用微调。评估采用SACTOR的静态分析引导式代理框架，通过结构感知的两阶段翻译、FFI端到端测试和多项代码质量指标进行验证。

Result: 报告计划比较微调模型、原始Qwen3-27B及其他模型的成功率，并统计Clippy警告数量、unsafe代码比例及具体失败模式。摘要未提供具体实验数值，因此无法判断各模型的定量提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该研究构建了一个从Rust知识增强、调试自修复到直接语义翻译的渐进式训练流程，并通过编译、静态分析和端到端测试进行综合评估，为提高LLM驱动的C到Rust迁移质量提供了系统化方案。

Abstract: Translating C code into safe, idiomatic Rust is a longstanding software-engineering goal because it can eliminate entire classes of memory-safety vulnerabilities while preserving the functional behavior of legacy systems. Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for this task but typically underperform when applied off-the-shelf, since general-purpose pretraining rarely emphasizes idiomatic Rust generation, cross-language semantic equivalence, or the ability to reason about and repair compiler/runtime feedback. In this report we describe a three-stage fine-tuning curriculum applied to Qwen3-27B that is designed to progressively specialize the model for the C-to-Rust (C2Rust) translation task: (1) continued pretraining on Rust-centric corpora to strengthen the model's prior over idiomatic Rust syntax and standard-library usage; (2) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on the microsoft/Verus_Training_Data dataset to instill debugging and self-repair behavior over Rust code; and (3) task-specific SFT on paired C/Rust solutions derived from LeetCode problems to teach direct semantic translation. We evaluate the resulting model using the agentic, static-analysis-guided verification framework of SACTOR, which performs structure-aware, two-phase (unidiomatic to idiomatic) translation with foreign-function-interface (FFI)-based end-to-end (E2E) testing. We report success rate, idiomaticity (Clippy lint counts, unsafe-code fraction), and failure-mode analyses, and compare our fine-tuned model against baseline Qwen3-27B and other LLMs evaluated under the same framework.

</details>


### [2] [Building AI-Intensive Software with AI: Early Results and a Cautionary Tale on Measuring Development Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13730)
*Victor Barros de Miranda Neves,Kiev Santos da Gama,Vinicius Cardoso Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该摘要报告了一项关于AI密集型软件开发真实成本的早期案例研究。研究团队发现，原先公布的19.4倍成本比因两类独立核算错误被高估约一倍，修正后约为9.9倍；核心贡献在于揭示此类错误具有隐蔽性、易发生性和可推广的方法学警示意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于AI密集型软件开发成本的实证报告较少，且成本口径、计价方式和反事实人力估算容易出错。研究旨在通过实际项目的持续观测，建立更可靠的成本模型，并识别可能导致结论显著偏差的常见核算陷阱。

Method: 研究以一个六人学生团队、历时一个学期开发对话式入职助手的项目为案例。系统包含基于检索增强生成的代码问答、引导式教程、依赖关系图和技术债分析等功能。作者采用三层成本模型：实际AI支出、自报人力投入和人力反事实成本；随后对初始结果进行复核，检查AI服务计费假设及反事实劳动力价格设定。

Result: 初始估计得到19.4倍成本比。复核发现两项彼此独立的错误：其一，在固定套餐订阅条件下错误推断按令牌计费成本；其二，使用了不适用的地区劳动力费率估算反事实人力成本。两项错误合计使成本比被高估约2倍，校正后的结果约为9.9倍。

Conclusion: AI密集型开发的成本评估不能仅依赖最终汇总数字，必须审计底层计费规则、劳动成本口径和反事实假设。该研究虽为早期单案例结果，但表明固定订阅成本分摊与地区化人力定价是高度易错且难以从最终数值察觉的环节。后续工作应形成可复现的成本定义、原始计费记录、敏感性分析和标准化反事实估算流程。

Abstract: Empirical reports on the true cost of AI-intensive software development remain scarce, and the few that exist are easy to get wrong in ways that never surface in the final number. We report early results from an ongoing case study: a six-person student team built a full conversational onboarding assistant -- RAG-based code chat, guided tours, dependency graphs, technical-debt analysis -- over one academic term using pervasive AI assistance. We instrumented development with a three-layer cost model (real AI spend, self-reported human effort, human counterfactual) and initially reported a 19.4x cost ratio. A follow-up pass revealed two independent errors -- inferring per-token cost under a flat-rate subscription, and pricing the counterfactual with the wrong regional labor rates -- that together had inflated the ratio by roughly 2x; the corrected figure is ~9.9x. We present this correction as an early, generalizable finding in its own right: both errors are easy to make, invisible in the final number, and plausibly common in similar reports. We outline next steps toward a more robust, replicable costing methodology for AI-intensive development.

</details>


### [3] [Does ISO-Grounded NFR Specification Improve LLM Code Generation? A Comparison of Rich and Structured Interventions against a Natural-Language Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13742)
*Joào Pedro Monteiro Pereira,Vinicius Cardoso Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文考察将大模型代码生成中的简短非功能需求扩展为基于 ISO/IEC 25010 的详细自然语言或结构化 JSON，是否优于单行提示。结果显示：标准化扩展能改善静态质量代理指标并降低提示措辞敏感性，但未稳定提升功能正确性；在语义内容相同的前提下，JSON 与自然语言形式差异极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成任务中的非功能需求通常过于简略，导致模型难以将性能、错误处理、代码异味和可读性等质量目标转化为稳定的实现行为。研究旨在验证以 ISO/IEC 25010 质量模型为基础的需求细化是否具有实际收益，并区分“需求语义内容”与“表达序列化形式”的影响。

Method: 研究在 HumanEval 与 HumanEval-ET 上比较三类提示：RobuNFR 风格的单行自然语言基线（NL-simple）、包含 ISO 质量语义的详细自然语言（NL-rich），以及承载相同 ISO 内容的 JSON（Structured）。覆盖性能、错误处理、代码异味、可读性四类需求；每种条件使用十个提示变体，在固定模型快照下生成代码，并采用配对非参数统计分析。评估维度包括功能正确性、扩展测试通过率、不可读性密度等静态质量代理指标及对提示措辞的敏感性。

Result: ISO 驱动的需求扩展总体降低了不可读性密度，且四类非功能需求均呈改善；例如性能需求下，NL-rich 将不可读性密度从 0.88 降至 0.69。扩展提示还降低了不同提示措辞导致的结果波动。但功能正确性没有出现可靠、稳定的提升。错误处理场景中，扩展测试通过率反而下降，表明防御式实现模式可能与精确输出导向的基准评价存在张力。在 ISO 语义内容保持一致时，NL-rich 与 Structured 的正确性差异可忽略，绝对差值不超过 0.023。

Conclusion: 对大模型代码生成而言，基于 ISO/IEC 25010 明确和充实非功能需求，主要能够提升静态质量表现与提示鲁棒性，但不能据此假定功能正确性同步提高。实践中应优先投入于标准驱动的需求语义设计，而非纠结于采用 JSON 还是自然语言表达；同时，错误处理等需求应结合基准的测试机制审慎设计，以避免质量导向实现与功能评测目标发生偏离。

Abstract: In LLM-based code generation, Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs) are often specified as terse one-line phrases. We ask whether grounding those specifications in ISO/IEC 25010 Quality Model, either as rich natural-language prose (NL-rich) or as structured JSON (Structured), improves code generated on HumanEval/HumanEval-ET compared to a RobuNFR-style one-line baseline (NL-simple). We evaluate four NFRs (performance, error handling, code smell, readability) with ten prompt variations per condition under a fixed model snapshot and paired non-parametric analysis. Primary finding: ISO-grounded enrichment improves static quality proxies (unreadability density falls across all four NFRs (e.g., Performance 0.88 -> 0.69 for NL-rich)) and reduces sensitivity to prompt wording, but does not reliably improve functional correctness; for error handling, extended-test pass rate decreases, suggesting tension between defensive coding patterns and exact-output benchmarks. Secondary finding: when ISO content is held constant, NL-rich and Structured differ negligibly in correctness (|delta| <= 0.023), indicating that semantic content matters more than JSON-vs-prose format. Practitioners should invest in standard-grounded NFR content rather than serialization form. A fully traceable replication package is provided.

</details>


### [4] [Engineering Reliable Coding Agents: Evaluating and Operating the System Around the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13867)
*Stephanie Jarmak*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将AI编码代理视为需要整体评估与运维的复杂系统，而非仅作为模型进行评价，系统分析执行环境、检索、记忆与状态管理、权限、审查界面及资源分配等因素对可靠性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估往往把端到端失败归因于模型能力，但实际问题可能来自任务构造、运行环境、状态管理、验证机制或可观测性等系统层面；单层改进也未必能转化为整体性能提升，因此需要系统级、可追溯的可靠性评估框架。

Method: 研究采用结构化多源综述，综合分析164篇学术文献、100条实践记录、29条基准记录和17条作者系统案例记录，并结合定向更新审计、软件工程覆盖率分析及分布式系统证据综合。研究还建立证据账本，记录已执行与未执行的检索路径、证据强度及其限制。

Result: 研究形成包含206条可靠性记录的版本化目录，其中包括193项门控实践、56项深度开发实践和13个研究方向；提出覆盖代理生命周期的依赖关系与修复不对称框架，提供运行中代理系统的测量与失败案例、可执行的评估和可靠性协议，以及5项带证据映射的可复用代理技能。结果显示，许多表面上的模型失败实际源于系统基础设施，且不同层面的优化可能无法传导至端到端结果。

Conclusion: AI编码代理的可靠性必须通过系统级方法进行评估和运营。应明确区分模型能力与基础设施效应，围绕任务、执行、检索、状态、验证和可观测性建立依赖链与防护机制，并设计能够在组件失效时安全恢复的系统。结论受工作负载、配置及证据覆盖范围影响，不能简单外推至所有场景。

Abstract: AI coding agents are commonly evaluated as models but deployed as systems. Their reliability depends not only on model capability, but on the harness, execution state, retrieval, memory and state management, permissions, review interfaces, and resource allocation. This monograph examines those boundaries and develops a framework for evaluating and operating coding agents reliably. It synthesizes 164 scholarly works, 100 practitioner records, 29 benchmark records, and 17 author-system case records through a structured multivocal review, targeted update audits, software-engineering coverage analysis, and distributed-systems evidence synthesis. Across this evidence, many apparent model failures originate elsewhere in the system, while improvements at one layer often fail to propagate to end-to-end outcomes. Evaluation and operation are treated as a dependency chain in which weaknesses in task construction, execution environments, retrieval, state management, verification, or observability can invalidate downstream conclusions. The monograph contributes a versioned catalog of 206 reliability records: 193 gated practices, including 56 developed in depth, plus 13 research leads; an evidence ledger; a framework for dependency and repair asymmetry across the agent lifecycle; measurements and failure cases from operated agent systems; runnable evaluation and reliability protocols; and five reusable agent skills with evidence maps. Together, these provide a system-level methodology for distinguishing model capability from infrastructure effects, designing defensible evaluations, and building systems that recover safely when components fail. The review is structured rather than exhaustive, evidence strength varies by topic, and results depend on workload and configuration. The methods record which search lanes were executed, which remain unexecuted, and limits on evidence-grading claims.

</details>


### [5] [Engineering Signals of Human-AI Collaboration in the Agentic Coding Era: A Longitudinal Analysis of 33,228 Pull Requests from vLLM and SGLang with Implications for Biomedical AI Agents and Bioinformatics Pipeline Developmen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13884)
*Jiada Li,Xuesong Ye,Olamide Olowoniyi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对vLLM和SGLang两个高活跃度AI基础设施项目进行纵向描述性分析，发现AI辅助开发时期项目吞吐量、贡献者参与度和人机协作信号显著增强，但PR规模总体稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程助手和自主代理开发系统快速普及，但关于其如何长期影响团队级开源软件工程活动的实证证据仍然有限。研究旨在刻画开发速度、协作模式和贡献者结构的变化。

Method: 研究统计了两个代码仓库中全部已合并PR，样本包括vLLM的18,290个PR和SGLang的14,938个PR。分析覆盖PR吞吐量、周期时间、贡献者多样性、评论密度、合并率、新作者参与度和PR规模七项指标，并按AI辅助软件开发的重要阶段划分四个时期，同时区分人工与机器人活动。该研究属于描述性纵向分析，未建立因果识别模型。

Result: 两个项目的PR吞吐量分别增长21倍和17.9倍，其中机器人PR对增长的贡献低于0.2%，表明增长主要由人工贡献驱动。最新时期的PR中位周期时间分别为1.04天和0.62天，P90周期时间分别为16.8天和14.3天。两项目的月度独立作者数持续增加；PR评论密度分别增长4.2倍和3.8倍，其中机器人评论估计贡献了增量的15%至20%。PR规模在不同时期相对稳定。

Conclusion: 在这两个高活跃度开源项目中，AI辅助开发与更高的开发吞吐量、更广泛的贡献者参与以及更强的人机协作信号同时出现。不过，由于研究仅为相关性描述，且样本集中于两个AI基础设施仓库，结果不能直接证明AI工具导致了这些变化，也不一定适用于其他项目或团队。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of AI coding assistants and autonomous agentic development systems has coincided with major changes in the pace and structure of open-source software engineering. Yet empirical longitudinal evidence of these changes at the team level remains limited. We present a descriptive longitudinal analysis of seven engineering metrics: pull request (PR) throughput, cycle time, contributor diversity, PR comment density, merge rate, new-author participation, and PR size. Metrics were computed from all merged PRs in two high-velocity AI infrastructure repositories, vLLM (February 2023-June 2026; 18,290 PRs) and SGLang (January 2024-June 2026; 14,938 PRs). We segment development into four eras aligned with major changes in AI-assisted software development and examine human- and bot-authored activities. Both projects show substantial increases in development velocity and AI-developer collaboration signals. PR throughput increased 21x in vLLM and 17.9x in SGLang, while bot-authored PRs accounted for less than 0.2% of this growth, indicating that the increase was overwhelmingly human-driven. In the latest era, median cycle time was 1.04 days for vLLM and 0.62 days for SGLang, while P90 cycle times reached 16.8 and 14.3 days, respectively. Monthly unique authors increased steadily in both projects, suggesting broader contributor participation. PR comment density increased 4.2x in vLLM and 3.8x in SGLang, with bot comments contributing an estimated 15-20% of the increase. In contrast, PR size remained relatively stable across eras. Overall, AI-assisted development is associated with higher throughput, broader contributor participation, and increased AI-developer collaboration signals in high-velocity open-source software development.

</details>


### [6] [Rethinking Automated Program Repair: The Impact of Bug Complexity, Fault Localization, and LLM Cost-efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14065)
*Junchi Liu,Ali Bigdeli,Roya Daneshi,Atu Ambala,Sudipto Ghosh,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估大语言模型驱动的自动程序修复在不同缺陷复杂度、故障定位精度、推理设置和成本条件下的表现，发现低成本模型也能修复大量中等复杂度缺陷，但修复效果与成本之间存在明显权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注自动程序修复的总体效果，对缺陷复杂度、故障定位误差、推理配置及经济成本如何影响修复性能缺乏系统分析，因此需要更全面的实证研究。

Method: 采用多维实证分析框架和统计分析，评估ChatRepair与CodeCorrector两种自动程序修复技术，并使用DeepSeek、GPT和Llama三类大语言模型，在不同缺陷复杂度和故障定位策略下比较其修复效果与成本效率。

Result: 结构复杂的缺陷和不精确的故障定位会增加修复难度，但基于大语言模型的技术仍具有竞争力。故障定位越不精确，不同修复技术之间的性能差距越明显。高成本模型和更强推理设置并不必然带来更高成本效率。超过50%的中等复杂度缺陷可由低成本模型修复；GPT-5比DeepSeek-V4-pro和DeepSeek-V3.2分别多修复7个和39个复杂缺陷，而DeepSeek-V3.2具有最佳总体成本效率。

Conclusion: 自动程序修复的评估不应仅关注修复数量，还应综合考虑缺陷复杂度、故障定位质量、推理配置和计算成本。低成本模型在实际应用中具有较高价值，但在复杂缺陷和低精度定位场景下，高性能模型可能提供更强的修复能力。

Abstract: Background: Software bugs remain a critical challenge in development, necessitating effective Automated Program Repair (APR) techniques. While Large Language Model (LLM)-based APR systems have shown promise, prior studies primarily focus on overall repair effectiveness. The effects of bug complexity, fault localization, reasoning settings, and repair cost-effectiveness remain insufficiently explored.
  Aims: This study presents a comprehensive empirical analysis of LLM-based APR, focusing on how repair performance is shaped by bug complexity, fault localization, reasoning settings, and costs.
  Method: We evaluate two APR techniques (ChatRepair and CodeCorrector) using three LLMs (DeepSeek, GPT, and Llama), and examine their performance across diverse levels of bug complexity and localization strategies through a multi-dimensional empirical framework and statistical analysis.
  Results: Although structurally complex bugs and imprecise fault localization make repair more challenging, LLM-based APR techniques still achieve competitive repair effectiveness. Imprecise fault localization can substantially enlarge the performance gap between APR techniques. Furthermore, higher-cost LLMs and stronger reasoning settings do not consistently yield better cost-efficiency, revealing a nontrivial trade-off between repair effectiveness and computational cost.
  Conclusions: Over 50% of moderately complex bugs can be repaired by low-cost LLM-based APR techniques. The repair effectiveness gap between APR techniques becomes larger as fault localization becomes less precise. GPT-5 repairs 7 and 39 more complex bugs than DeepSeek-V4-pro and DeepSeek-V3.2, respectively; whereas the total repair cost of DeepSeek-V3.2 shows the best cost-efficiency performance.

</details>


### [7] [DepWareTrans: Dependency-Aware Incremental Repository Migration across Co-executable Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14128)
*Sivajeet Chand,Alexander Pretschner,Steve Haupt,Derui Zhu,Sushant Kumar Pandey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向代码仓库级迁移的依赖感知增量翻译框架，将翻译单元从单个文件提升为依赖一致的文件批次，从而显著提高跨语言迁移的编译与测试成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的代码翻译方法通常以文件为单位，难以处理复杂的跨文件依赖。在工业系统STAR从Java迁移至Kotlin的场景中，文件级翻译会产生结果碎片化、依赖不一致和端到端正确性不足等问题；单纯依赖迭代反馈也难以有效修复这些错误。

Method: 首先构建代码仓库的跨文件依赖图，识别并聚合相互依赖的文件，形成依赖一致的翻译批次；随后对每个批次执行增量翻译，并结合编译验证和测试验证进行迭代修正。该方法在Java-Kotlin、Java-Scala和C#-F#等语言对上进行评估，同时覆盖开源仓库和工业系统。

Result: 在51K行代码的STAR工业仓库上，文件级方法的编译成功率为38.16%、测试成功率为9.39%；依赖感知批处理方法在评估设置下均达到100%的编译和测试成功率，并在较少迭代次数内收敛。实证研究表明，跨文件依赖未解析是仓库级翻译失败的主要原因。

Conclusion: 以依赖一致批次为基本翻译单元能够有效缓解跨文件依赖导致的错误，相较文件级翻译显著提升仓库级代码迁移的可扩展性、可靠性和端到端正确性。

Abstract: Repository-level code translation is critical for modernizing legacy systems, yet existing approaches based on large language models (LLMs) operate at the file level and fail to scale to codebases with complex inter-file dependencies. This limitation is evident in our industrial setting, where we aim to migrate a production repository (STAR) from Java to Kotlin, but file-level approaches produce fragmented results and fail to achieve end-to-end correctness. In this paper, we show that the primary cause of failure at the repository level is dependency inconsistency. Through an empirical study on open-source and industrial systems, we find that most errors arise from unresolved cross-file dependencies that cannot be effectively addressed by iterative feedback alone. We propose a dependency-aware incremental migration framework that elevates the unit of translation from individual files to dependency-consistent batches. Our approach constructs a dependency graph, groups interdependent files, and performs batched translation with iterative compile- and test-driven validation. We evaluate our method on a 51K line of code (LOC) industrial system and multiple repositories across interoperable language pairs (Java-Kotlin, Java-Scala, and C#-F#). On the STAR repository, file-level approaches achieve 38.16% compilation and 9.39% test success, whereas our approach achieves 100% compilation and test success across the evaluated settings, converging within a small number of iterations. These results show that dependency-aware batching improves scalability and reliability in repository-level code translation.

</details>


### [8] [LegacyWorld: Atomicity-Aware Evaluation of GUI Agents for Legacy Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14131)
*Thilo Reintjes,Sivajeet Chand,Derui Zhu,Sushant Kumar Pandey,Alexander Pretschner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估多模态大语言模型计算机使用代理在遗留企业系统GUI工作流自动化中的可靠性，重点提出并实证分析“原子性”：任务要么正确完成，要么失败且不留下非预期持久化副作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留系统通常缺乏可编程接口，关键流程仍依赖人工GUI操作。仅凭成功演示无法证明代理适用于真实业务，因为失败运行可能对业务或医疗记录造成持久且无效的修改。因此，需要面向安全失败和状态一致性的系统化评估方法。

Method: 研究构建了一个由领域专家参与设计的基准，包含28个Windows GUI工作流。每个工作流定义初始状态、目标状态和任务专用验证器，并以原子性作为核心评价标准。研究比较了专家编写的提示词与依据专家黄金路径屏幕录制自动生成的提示词，并在六种托管式计算机使用代理上进行评测。

Result: 实验表明，有效完成、安全失败和产生非原子副作用是三种不同的运行表现，代理不能仅依据任务完成率进行评价。屏幕录制生成的提示词和专家提示词均被纳入比较，但摘要未报告具体性能数值。结果支持使用状态验证器识别持久化副作用，并区分真正可靠的完成与表面上的成功。

Conclusion: 面向遗留工作流的AI自动化应将工作流捕获、状态验证器和具备原子性感知的验收测试作为一等需求。部署前评估不仅要衡量完成能力，还必须验证失败时系统能否保持无非预期状态变化。

Abstract: Legacy and legacy-like enterprise systems often remain difficult to modernize because critical workflows expose limited programmable interfaces and still require manual GUI interaction. This paper reports a pre-deployment evaluation study motivated by the development of legacy-use, an industry-oriented framework for automating such workflows with multimodal LLM agents. During framework development, domain experts helped identify stateful workflows where successful demos are not sufficient: a failed agent run may still leave persistent invalid changes in business or healthcare records. We therefore evaluate computer-use agents using atomicity: a run should either complete the intended workflow correctly or fail without unintended persistent side effects. We construct a domain-expert-informed benchmark of 28 Windows GUI workflows, each specified with an initial state, goal state, and task-specific validator. We compare expert-crafted prompts with prompts generated from screen recordings of expert golden-path executions. Across six hosted computer-use agents, our results show that useful completion, safe failure, and non-atomic side effects are distinct operational profiles. We conclude that workflow capture, state validators, and atomicity-aware acceptance tests should be first-class requirements for AI-based legacy workflow automation.

</details>


### [9] [Breaking Models to Test the Judge: A Mutation Testing Approach for Semantic Evaluators of Domain Class Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14315)
*Kevin Delcourt,Meriem Ben Chaaben,Abdelhamid Rouatbi,Luciano Marchezan,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向语义评判器的变异测试方法，通过向领域类图中注入受控语义缺陷，评估LLM等评判器识别模型与文本描述不一致性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义建模任务通常缺乏唯一真实答案，人工评估成本高且具有主观性，因此需要一种可扩展、可重复的评判器有效性评估方法。

Method: 以PlantUML领域类图及其文本系统描述为基础，设计11种变异算子（如删除类）生成含缺陷的类图变体；随后在六种评判器配置上进行测试，包括三种LLM和两类提示词变体，并将自动变异测试结果与人工评估结果进行比较。

Result: 自动变异测试在识别性能更优的评判器配置方面，与人工评估总体保持较高一致性，表明该方法能够有效反映不同语义评判器的相对性能。

Conclusion: 变异测试可作为分析语义评判器的可扩展代理方法，尤其适用于缺乏唯一标准答案且人工判断成本较高的语义建模任务。

Abstract: In software engineering, many semantic modeling tasks lack a unique ground truth, as human judgments are both costly and subjective. This paper explores mutation testing as a scalable alternative for evaluating semantic judges (e.g., LLM-based) of models. We propose a mutation testing approach in which controlled semantic defects are injected into domain class diagrams. Starting from pairs of PlantUML class diagrams and textual system descriptions, we apply mutation operators (e.g., removing a class) to generate faulty variants. A candidate judge is then evaluated based on its ability to detect the injected defects. We define 11 mutation operators for the task of comparing a domain class diagram against a textual description and evaluate the proposed approach against a conventional manual assessment of judgment validity. Across six judge configurations (three LLMs and two prompt variants), the automated mutation testing approach is largely consistent with the manual assessment in identifying the better-performing configurations. The results suggest that mutation testing may serve as a scalable proxy for analyzing semantic judges.

</details>


### [10] [ATLAS: Discovering Agent Strategies through LLM-Guided Abstraction and Automata Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14352)
*Ignacio D. Lopez-Miguel,Andreas Happe,Jürgen Cito,Ezio Bartocci,Bettina Könighofer,Martin Tappler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATLAS通过轨迹抽象与自动机学习，从基于大语言模型的智能体交互轨迹中恢复可解释的有限状态行为模型，用于策略发现、行为分析、审计、探索和知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估主要关注任务是否成功及执行轨迹，难以揭示智能体采用的策略、关键决策点和失败循环。研究旨在将难以理解的原始轨迹转化为可分析、可解释的显式行为模型。

Method: ATLAS首先对智能体—环境交互轨迹进行抽象，再结合自动机学习推断有限状态模型，以表示智能体观察到的行为策略。研究进一步利用模型开展重复行为、成功路径、决策点和失败循环分析，并通过符号化模型变换生成简洁解释、指导探索，以及将强模型的行为知识迁移至更紧凑的语言模型。

Result: 在基于大语言模型的安全评估智能体上进行概念验证，所得模型揭示了原始执行轨迹难以直接发现的高层行为策略。针对12台存在不同缺陷的机器，模型变换能够生成简洁的行为解释，并支持从强大模型向紧凑语言模型的符号化知识迁移。

Conclusion: ATLAS证明，将智能体轨迹转换为显式自动机模型能够提升对智能体行为的系统性理解和可解释性，并为审计、模型引导探索、失败分析和行为知识迁移提供统一基础。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used for complex tasks such as software testing and cybersecurity assessment. While these agents demonstrate impressive capabilities, their behavior is difficult to understand, explain, and analyze. Existing evaluations focus mainly on task success and execution traces, offering limited insight into the strategies employed by the agent. We present ATLAS (Automata Learning for Agent Trajectory Analysis and Strategy Discovery), an approach for recovering interpretable behavioral models from agent trajectories. ATLAS combines trace abstraction with automata learning to infer finite-state models that capture observed agent-environment interaction strategies. These models provide human-interpretable insights and support automated analyses of recurring behaviors, decision points, successful task-completion paths, and failure loops. As a proof of concept, we apply ATLAS to trajectories generated by an LLM-based penetration-testing agent. The resulting models expose high-level behavioral strategies for exploiting vulnerable machines that are difficult to identify from raw execution traces alone. We discuss how learned behavioral models can support explainability, model-guided exploration, auditing, and analysis of agentic systems. We further demonstrate symbolic model-based knowledge transfer from powerful frontier models to compact language models. In addition, we show how model transformations can derive concise explanations of agent behavior in a penetration-testing case study comprising 12 vulnerable machines. ATLAS highlights a new opportunity for model-driven engineering: transforming agent trajectories into explicit behavioral models that enable systematic understanding and analysis of otherwise opaque AI agents.

</details>

### [11] [A Barrier-Free Synchronization Algorithm for Multi-Engine AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13757)
*Chungha Sung,Nikil V. Shyamsunder,Hanliang Zhang,Daniel Kroening,Joonwon Choi*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向多计算引擎AI加速器的无全局屏障同步算法：通过运行时跟踪循环迭代次数并计算动态完成阈值，在任意嵌套、动态边界的结构化循环中精确维护跨引擎数据依赖。该方案已在AWS Neuron ISA编译器后端实现，并经形式化验证，在机器学习内核上显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多引擎加速器可并行执行不同专用计算任务，但编译器必须保证消费者指令仅在生产者数据完成后发射。直线代码可静态计算完成计数阈值；循环，尤其是动态次数和嵌套循环，会使该阈值无法静态确定。现有做法通常在迭代边界插入全引擎屏障以重置同步状态，虽易于实现，却会阻塞本可并行的任务并损失性能。

Method: 方法以循环迭代计数为运行时状态，针对每条跨引擎依赖计算动态同步阈值，而不是在循环边界强制全局屏障。算法面向任意嵌套、动态有界的结构化控制流，精确决定消费者所需等待的生产者完成数量。作者将其实现为AWS Neuron ISA层面的编译器后端优化传递，并从正确性角度形式化定义“满足依赖所需的最小同步”，再利用Lean证明助手通过双模拟证明算法达到该最小准则。

Result: 相较于基于屏障的基线方案，该方法在一组机器学习内核上将延迟降低10%至45%；在同步开销主导的微基准测试中获得3.3倍加速；其性能通常可达到或超过人工调优的计算引擎分配方案。形式化证明表明，算法既不会因过早发射消费者而破坏依赖，也不会因过度等待引入非必要停顿。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，多引擎AI加速器中的循环同步无需依赖迭代边界的全局屏障。基于运行时循环计数的动态阈值能够以最小必要同步保持正确性，同时释放循环内部与跨迭代并行度。其编译器实现、实测性能收益及Lean形式化验证共同说明，该方法具备作为多引擎编译后端同步机制的实用价值。

Abstract: Multi-engine AI accelerators such as AWS Trainium comprise specialized compute engines that execute in parallel, and the compiler must synchronize the data dependencies between them. For straight-line code this is simple: each dependency reduces to waiting for a threshold count of instruction completions, which the compiler computes statically. Loops admit no such static threshold; a simple solution inserts all-engine barriers at iteration boundaries, resetting synchronization state so each loop body can be treated as straight-line, at the cost of parallelism.
  We present a barrier-free synchronization algorithm that instead enforces each dependency precisely across structured control flow with arbitrarily nested, dynamically bounded loops. The key idea is to compute dynamic thresholds at runtime from tracked loop iteration counts.
  We implemented it as a compiler backend pass at the AWS Neuron ISA level. On a suite of ML kernels, it reduces latency 10-45% relative to the barrier-based baseline, achieves a 3.3x speedup on a synchronization-bound microbenchmark, and often matches or exceeds hand-tuned manual allocation.
  Issuing a consumer too early violates its dependency, while issuing too late unnecessarily stalls execution. We formally characterize the minimum synchronization required for correctness and verify in the Lean proof assistant, via bisimulation, that our algorithm meets this criterion.

</details>


### [12] [GPU Offload in Rust: Portable, Safe, and Fast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13759)
*Manuel S. Drehwald,Marcelo Domínguez,Kevin Sala,Alán Aspuru-Guzik,Johannes Doerfert*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种原生集成于Rust编译器与LLVM后端的零开销、多厂商GPU编译框架，在保持内存安全的同时实现高性能GPU内核执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU高性能编程通常需要在执行效率与内存安全之间权衡：安全方案往往依赖厂商特定DSL，而通用方案则需要使用不安全的原始指针。本文旨在将Rust的所有权、类型系统和别名保证扩展到大规模并行GPU环境，并避免性能损失。

Method: 框架利用Rust类型系统、所有权模型和noalias严格别名保证，通过LLVM Offload基础设施管理和优化主机与设备间的数据传输。针对主机与设备目标之间的ABI降低不匹配问题，设计了双阶段编译流水线，以安全处理手动及编译器生成的内存移动，并支持多厂商GPU后端。

Result: 在RAJAPerf基准上的评估表明，基于rustc的方案能够生成具有竞争力的GPU内核LLVM IR，并在内核性能方面达到接近原生、手工优化CUDA和HIP C++实现的水平。摘要未提供具体性能数值或开销数据。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，Rust的内存安全机制可以与多厂商GPU编译及高性能执行结合，在不依赖显式不安全原始指针的情况下实现具有竞争力的性能，为安全且可移植的GPU编程提供了可行路径。

Abstract: High-performance GPU programming has traditionally forced a compromise between execution efficiency and memory safety. While Rust guarantees compile-time memory safety for host CPUs via its strict ownership model, applying these constraints to massively parallel GPU execution environments has previously mandated either vendor-locked Domain-Specific Languages (DSLs) or escaping to explicit unsafe raw pointers. This paper presents a zero-overhead, multi-vendor GPU compilation framework built natively into the Rust compiler (rustc) and LLVM backends.
  We leverage Rust's rich type system, ownership system, and strict aliasing guarantees (noalias) to efficiently manage and optimize data transfers through LLVM's Offload infrastructure. We expose the technical challenges of cross-vendor ABI lowering mismatches between Host and Device targets and introduce a two-pass compilation pipeline capable of safely handling both manual and compiler-generated memory movements. Evaluating our framework on RAJAPerf demonstrates that our rustc-based solution can generate competitive LLVM IR for GPU kernels, achieving a solid kernel performance against native, hand-optimized CUDA and HIP C++ baselines.

</details>


### [13] [Memory Allocation for Constant-Bounded Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14471)
*Vinícius Silva,Kael Soares,Márcio Costa e Fernando Magno Quintão Pereira*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文研究常数有界程序的内存分配问题，提出结合树扫描与内存整理的分配策略，可在多种场景下近似最优地降低栈和堆内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常数有界程序的执行长度对所有输入均受语法限制，适用于经验证的内核扩展、加密例程和固定结构机器学习模型。现有朴素分配策略可能造成严重内存浪费，因此需要一种具有理论保证且可高效实现的分配方法。

Method: 将控制流表示为树，并采用树扫描分配策略；同时引入内存碎片整理以复用已释放空间。该方法保证内存占用至多为最大活跃内存加最大缓冲区大小，并在允许原地交换时达到最优。系统实现包括：在Elixir到eBPF编译器中作为栈溢出优化器，以及针对采用Structured Control-Flow方言的有界MLIR程序的静态堆分配器。

Result: 在真实eBPF工作负载上，栈空间减少超过90%。即使发生激进的代码扩展，碎片整理通常也很少需要，整体内存占用仍仅为朴素分配策略所需内存的一小部分。

Conclusion: 常数有界性能够支持对最优栈使用量进行紧致且多项式时间的近似。所提出的分配器兼具理论内存上界、实际可部署性和显著的空间节省效果，适合栈受限及静态内存管理场景。

Abstract: This work studies memory allocation for constant-bounded programs, whose execution length is syntactically limited for all inputs. Examples of such programs include verified kernel extensions, cryptographic routines, and fixed-shape machine-learning models. We show that constant boundedness enables a tight, polynomial-time approximation of optimal stack usage by viewing control flow as a tree and applying a tree-scan allocation strategy augmented with memory defragmentation. Our approach guarantees memory usage bounded by the maximum live memory plus, at most, the size of the largest buffer, and is optimal when in-place swapping is permitted. We deploy the proposed allocator in two scenarios. First, in an Elixir-to-eBPF compiler, as a spiller that optimizes stack space. Second, as a static heap allocator for bounded MLIR programs using the Structured Control-Flow dialect. Results demonstrate stack reductions exceeding 90% on real eBPF workloads and show that, even under aggressive code expansion, defragmentation is rarely required and memory usage remains a small fraction of that required by naive allocation strategies.

</details>

### [14] [Simplicial Semantics for Belief Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13763)
*Philip Sink*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文在单纯形语义中定义信念修正，将原先赋予面（facets）的原子公式改为赋予节点，并据此提出基于世界相似性的两种修正形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有单纯形信念语义将原子公式赋予面，难以充分表达节点作为特定主体视角的概念。将节点解释为视角后，可把由多个视角组成的世界之间的相似性形式化，从而为信念修正提供基础。

Method: 以既有单纯形复形信念语义为基线，构造节点标注原子公式的新语义；将共享视角的数量作为世界之间的接近度或相似性依据；在此基础上给出两种信念修正的形式化定义，并分析迭代修正中的概念问题。

Result: 论文建立了节点赋值的单纯形信念修正框架，提出了两种基于世界接近关系的修正方案。同时发现，在迭代修正过程中，这些定义可能产生概念陷阱，因此提出引入主体记忆机制作为潜在解决方向。

Conclusion: 将节点视为主体视角、将面视为由视角组成的世界，能够自然支持单纯形语义中的信念修正与世界相似性建模。但要使迭代修正保持概念一致性，还需要记录此前已公开或修正的信息。

Abstract: This paper will give a definition of belief revision within simplicial semantics. Starting from the work presented in "A Semantics for Belief in Simplicial Complexes" as a baseline, this paper modifies the semantics for belief given there to allow atomic formulae to be assigned to nodes, not facets. Conceptually and philosophically, this version of the semantics is better suited if one wishes to interpret the nodes of a simplicial model of epistemic logic as a "perspective" assigned to a particular agent. If nodes are perspectives, it then follows that worlds, or the facets of a simplicial model, are composed themselves of perspectives. This allows us to say that two worlds are more similar, or "nearer", if they share more perspectives. With this conceptual notion of nearness in hand, two different formal presentations of revision are given. We conclude by exploring some conceptual pitfalls surrounding these definitions under iterated revision, and motivate a few potential solutions that involve giving the agents a "memory" of what has been announced so far.

</details>


### [15] [Rewrite Once, Validate Anywhere: Producing OWL-Aware SHACL Constraints (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14104)
*Anouk Oudshoorn,Piotr Gorczyca,Dörthe Arndt*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将OWL EL⁻本体推理内化到SHACL约束中的重写方法，使不具备推理能力的标准SHACL验证器也能完成等价的约束验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDF图通常依赖OWL本体中的隐含知识。传统流程需要先使用推理器推理，再使用验证器检查约束，涉及不同技术组件，效率较低且容易产生配置或处理错误。

Method: 构造一个重写过程，以SHACL形状和OWL EL⁻本体为输入，将本体公理转换并整合为SHACL约束。生成的约束可由支持SHACL Core的验证器直接执行，并在理论上保持与“先推理、后验证”流程相同的结果。

Result: 实验将该方法与推理器和验证器串联执行的方案，以及内置推理支持的验证器进行比较。基准测试表明，所提出的方法通常能更高效地发现约束违规。

Conclusion: 将OWL推理知识编译进SHACL约束能够简化推理与验证的组合，降低对专用推理支持的依赖，并在保持验证结果一致性的同时提升违规检测效率。

Abstract: The Shapes Constraint Language (SHACL) is a W3C recommendation to express syntactic constraints, called shapes, on RDF graphs. SHACL validators are used to test whether a given graph adheres to such a shape. However, RDF graphs often come with OWL ontologies, whose implicit knowledge needs to be taken into account. This is classically handled by first applying reasoning and then performing the constraint checking on the results, often using different technologies which makes the process inefficient and vulnerable for mistakes.
  To overcome this, we propose to internalise the OWL axioms in the SHACL constraints; we construct a rewriting which takes as input both shapes and an OWL EL$^-$ ontology -- a fragment of OWL EL restricting the usage of existential restrictions -- and produces SHACL constraints. This output can then be evaluated by any validator supporting SHACL core regardless of its reasoning support, while yielding the same results as the traditional approach. The implementation of our translation is evaluated both against applying state-of-the-art reasoners and validators consecutively, as against validators with built-in reasoning support. For our benchmark, we show that our approach is in general more efficient in finding violations compared to the sequential approach, thus providing a powerful tool which simplifies combining reasoning with validation.

</details>


### [16] [Bisimulations and Modal Logics for Higher Dimensional Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14117)
*Safa Zouari,Rob van Glabbeek,Krzysztof Ziemiański*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文为高维自动机（HDA）建立了一套刻画并区分并发行为等价性的模态逻辑框架，最终实现对历史保持历史双模拟（hhp-bisimilarity）的逻辑刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HDA能够几何化表示真正并发，但此前尚无模态逻辑能够刻画hhp-bisimilarity这一行为等价谱中最精细的等价关系，因此需要构造相应的中间等价与逻辑体系。

Method: 引入严格位于ST-bisimilarity与hhp-bisimilarity之间的若干中间等价关系；通过区分路径的相似性与包含关系，给出这些等价关系的简洁定义；在此基础上构造模态逻辑，并研究其自然逻辑片段。

Result: 所提出的模态逻辑能够刻画HDA上的hhp-bisimilarity；其自然片段分别能够刻画ST-bisimilarity及所引入的中间等价关系。

Conclusion: 路径相似性与路径包含关系的分离为HDA行为等价性的分层描述提供了统一框架，并首次实现了hhp-bisimilarity的模态逻辑刻画。

Abstract: Higher-Dimensional Automata (HDAs) provide a geometric model of true concurrency. While hereditary history-preserving (hhp) bisimilarity is the finest behavioural equivalence in van Glabbeek's spectrum, no modal logic has previously characterised it on HDAs. We introduce several new intermediate equivalences that sit strictly between ST- and hhp-bisimilarity. We show how separating similarity and subsumption of paths leads to a clean formulation of these equivalences, and we present a modal logic that characterises hhp-bisimilarity. Natural fragments characterise ST-bisimilarity and the intermediate notions.

</details>

*Mikoláš Janota,Joao Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文将隐式击中集枚举方法引入量化布尔公式求解，先处理两层量词情形，再推广为通用算法；实验表明该方法具有较强竞争力，并常优于现有求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式击中集枚举已在最大可满足性等任务中表现出应用价值，但其在量化布尔公式上的系统化应用仍有待发展。论文旨在建立两者的联系，并利用该思路提升量化布尔公式求解性能。

Method: 首先提出一个针对两层量词量化布尔公式的简单算法，分析其与既有隐式击中集枚举方法及基于抽象细化的量化布尔公式方法之间的关联；随后将核心思想扩展，提出一种推广隐式击中集枚举概念的新型通用量化布尔公式算法。

Result: 在具有代表性的基准实例上，所提出的新算法与当前先进量化布尔公式求解方法相比具有竞争力，且在不少实例上取得更优表现。

Conclusion: 隐式击中集枚举可作为量化布尔公式求解的有效算法框架。通过从两层量词问题推广到更一般的量化结构，论文提供了一种性能突出的新求解思路，并揭示了其与抽象细化方法的概念联系。

Abstract: Algorithms based on the enumeration of implicit hitting sets find a growing number of applications, which include maximum satisfiability and model based diagnosis, among others.
  This paper exploits enumeration of implicit hitting sets in the context of Quantified Boolean Formulas (QBF). The paper starts by developing a simple algorithm for QBF with two levels of quantification, which is shown to relate with existing work on enumeration of implicit hitting sets, but also with recent work on QBF based on abstraction refinement. The paper then extends these ideas and develops a novel QBF algorithm, which generalizes the concept of enumeration of implicit hitting sets.
  Experimental results, obtained on representative problem instances, show that the novel algorithm is competitive with, and often outperforms, the state of the art in QBF solving.

</details>


### [18] [Solving QBF with Counterexample Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14322)
*Mikoláš Janota,William Klieber,Joao Marques-Silva,Edmund Clarke*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出两种将反例引导的抽象精化（CEGAR）用于量化布尔公式（QBF）求解器的方法，并通过实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QBF求解器主要依赖DPLL等搜索机制，亟需探索新的搜索框架与学习机制，以提升对复杂QBF实例的求解能力。

Method: 第一种方法设计由CEGAR驱动的递归求解算法，以替代DPLL驱动的搜索；第二种方法将CEGAR作为额外的学习技术，集成到已有的DPLL型QBF求解器中，并实现原型进行实验评估。

Result: 实验表明，CEGAR驱动的求解器在QBF-LIB的若干实例族上优于现有求解器；同时，DPLL求解器能够从引入的CEGAR学习机制中获得性能收益。

Conclusion: CEGAR为QBF求解提供了两条有前景的研究方向：构建独立的CEGAR驱动型求解器，以及将其作为新型学习机制集成到DPLL框架中。

Abstract: We propose two novel approaches for using Counterexample-Guided Abstraction Refinement (CEGAR) in Quantified Boolean Formula (QBF) solvers. The first approach develops a recursive algorithm whose search is driven by CEGAR (rather than by DPLL). The second approach employs CEGAR as an additional learning technique in an existing DPLL-based QBF solver. Experimental evaluation of the implemented prototypes shows that the CEGAR-driven solver outperforms existing solvers on a number of families in the QBF-LIB and that the DPLL solver benefits from the additional type of learning. Thus this article opens two promising avenues in QBF: CEGAR-driven solvers as an alternative to existing approaches and a novel type of learning in DPLL.

</details>

### [19] ["I Thought You Were The Uncensored Place": Norms, Rules, and Moderation in AI-Generated Sexual Content Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13659)

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: As AI-generated sexual content (AIG-SC) is increasingly produced, online communities have emerged to support creators' needs. To understand whether and how community governance attempts work to prevent abuse while supporting free expression, we interviewed 24 members and moderators of large AIG-SC online communities (10,000+ members) with stated rules against creating and sharing abusive content (e.g., AI-generated CSAM). Through in-depth interviews, we offer insight into: (1) how and why these communities form; (2) implicit community norms; (3) explicitly stated rules---and their operationalization via content moderation; and (4) tensions between community values and moderation that leave space for abusive behavior.
  Our findings reveal a complex picture: while many creators and communities have personal boundaries against abuse, advice and resources for creating any form of AI-generated sexual content are accessible to users regardless of their intentions. Further complicating community moderation are norms that center anti-censorship and non-judgment, which leave moderators to justify their actions using the limits of the law and terms of service. We end by reflecting on the ways in which technical, community, and legal governance may most effectively mitigate the production of abusive content.

</details>


### [20] [Weird Machines in Transport Layer Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13685)
*Michael Collins,Jada Cumberland,Brianne Dunn,Ross Gore,Samuel Jackson,Sachin Shetty,Jonathan Takeshita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文将“怪异机器”理论扩展到TLS握手，主张OpenSSL与BoringSSL中的会话缓存、重协商、扩展解析和证书验证等正常机制可组合为图灵完备计算系统，并将其影响定义为与认证和信任决策耦合的“信任致动”。作者声称以真实代码路径实现了握手异常检测与策略绕过两项验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有怪异机器研究主要聚焦软件架构组件及部分网络化物理系统，而TLS通常被视为安全协议实现。论文旨在说明：即使不依赖内存破坏或外部恶意程序，协议状态机、持久会话状态和重试逻辑的组合也可能形成可编程行为，从而影响认证与密码策略等高价值信任决策。

Method: 作者提出信任致动的形式化概念，将计算结果与TLS认证、证书验证及密码策略判断的结果关联；随后给出图灵完备性条件，即实现具备会话存储、序列计数器算术、基于握手状态的条件分支，以及经由会话恢复或重试循环形成的迭代能力。最后在Docker中的真实OpenSSL服务器与客户端二进制上，沿实际代码路径构建两个概念验证。

Result: 摘要报告两个验证结果：其一，利用标准TLS原语组成哨兵式防御机制，用于发现异常握手行为；其二，利用同类原语和连接中途重协商路径，绕过密码强度策略检查。论文强调该现象不依赖内存安全缺陷。摘要未提供受影响版本、触发前提、形式化证明细节、实验覆盖范围、误报率或修复效果，因此无法仅据摘要判断结论的普适性与实际风险等级。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是把TLS实现中的状态管理与控制流视为潜在的通用计算基底，并指出此类计算可直接改变信任决策。若论证成立，TLS实现的安全审计不应只关注传统内存漏洞，还应审查跨握手阶段的状态组合、重协商一致性、会话恢复约束以及策略检查是否在状态转换后被重新强制执行。

Abstract: Weird machines are latent computational capabilities that emerge from the composition of architectural components. Prior work has studied this phenomenon extensively in software systems, including x86 instructions, ELF metadata, and page tables, and more recently in cyber-physical systems such as industrial control networks. This paper extends weird machine theory to a new domain: the Transport Layer Security (TLS) handshake and its two dominant implementations, OpenSSL and BoringSSL.
  We show that legitimate TLS primitives, including session cache entries, renegotiation logic, extension parsing, and certificate verification steps, compose into Turing-complete systems whose computation is coupled to authentication and trust decisions rather than physical actuation. We formalize this coupling, which we call trust actuation, and argue that any TLS implementation providing session storage, arithmetic on sequence counters, conditional branching on handshake state, and iteration through resumption or retry loops satisfies the conditions for arbitrary computation.
  We validate this theory with two working demonstrations built on real OpenSSL code paths. The first, a sentinel system, composes standard TLS primitives into a defensive mechanism that detects anomalous handshake behavior. The second, an authentication bypass, composes the same class of primitives into an attack that defeats a cipher-strength policy check through mid-connection renegotiation, without any memory corruption or external malware. Both demonstrations run against real server and client binaries in Docker.

</details>


### [21] [A Reproducibility Protocol for Cross-Implementation Evaluation of Post-Quantum ACVP Test Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13784)
*Christopher M. Frost*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究提出并执行了一个面向三种公开实现的、产品中立的 NIST ML-KEM 可复现性协议。在固定 ACVP 测试语料和严格证据分类下，所有可执行记录均通过，重叠测试结果完全一致，但不同实现支持的确定性与验证测试范围存在明显差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码标准的独立实现应产生一致的已知答案，但一致性只有在测试语料、版本、接口、排除项和证据定义明确时才具有解释力。研究旨在建立可复核的跨实现比较方法，并揭示公开 ML-KEM 软件包在测试能力覆盖方面的差异。

Method: 固定公开的 NIST ACVP 语料库和协议版本，冻结提供方能力边界，统一验证错误分类，保留全部选定测试记录，并分别记录字节结果、验证判定、不支持操作和适配器错误。对 @noble/post-quantum 0.7.0、liboqs 0.16.0 和 Go 1.26.4 进行源码构建实验，重复三次，覆盖 2,160 条基础记录；另设 keyGen-ek-projection 诊断和三个冻结控制用例。

Result: 1,650 条声明可执行记录全部与 NIST 预期结果一致，510 条不支持记录全部符合 Go 预先声明的能力边界。可执行重叠测试中，noble-liboqs 完成 720/720 条一致，Go 与另外两者的每组比较均为 210/210。一项独立诊断中 Go 的 150 条封装公钥投影全部匹配。三个控制用例均产生预先定义的准确结果，未发现基础失败、适配器错误或状态不稳定。

Conclusion: 结果支持在明确边界内的作者运行可重复性，并表明三种公开 ML-KEM 实现的确定性测试和验证测试接口存在实质差异。研究未证明外部独立复现、标准认证、穷尽正确性、侧信道安全、集成安全性或生产环境保障。

Abstract: Independent implementations of a cryptographic standard should reproduce the same known-answer results, yet agreement is meaningful only when the corpus, revisions, public interfaces, exclusions, and evidence are precisely stated. This study defines a product-neutral reproducibility protocol for three public implementations of NIST ML-KEM against a pinned public Automated Cryptographic Validation Protocol corpus. Protocol v2 freezes provider-specific capabilities, applies one validation-error taxonomy symmetrically, preserves every selected case, and separates bytes, validation verdicts, unsupported operations, and adapter errors. The source-built experiment evaluated @noble/post-quantum 0.7.0, liboqs 0.16.0, and Go 1.26.4. Across three repetitions, the required Cartesian product comprised 2,160 base records: all 1,650 declared executable evaluations matched the NIST oracle, and all 510 unsupported records matched Go's predeclared capability boundary. Pairwise agreement was complete on every executable overlap: 720 of 720 noble-liboqs records and 210 of 210 records for each Go pairing. A separate keyGen-ek-projection diagnostic matched all 150 Go encapsulation-key projections without counting them as full key generation. Three frozen controls independently exercised byte comparison, verdict comparison, and malformed-response error separation; each produced its exact predeclared outcome. No base failure, adapter error, or status instability occurred. The evidence establishes bounded author-run repeatability and exposes a practical standards gap: public ML-KEM packages provide materially different deterministic and validation-test surfaces. Independent external reproduction remains unobserved. The results do not establish certification, exhaustive correctness, side-channel resistance, secure integration, or production assurance.

</details>


### [22] [The ack3 H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset: Audit Scope Across 135 Security Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13792)
*Josef Gattermayer,Jan Kalivoda,Arman Bašović*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究分析了135起DeFi安全事件，发现“项目曾接受审计”并不等同于“实际攻击路径已被审计覆盖”。在有可识别审计记录的68起事件中，67.6%的攻击路径位于所有已知公开审计范围之外，并对应94.4%的报告损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约审计通常只针对特定代码、版本和时间点，但市场和项目方常将“已审计”理解为项目整体安全保证。研究旨在区分项目级审计历史与具体事件路径的审计覆盖范围，并评估二者之间的差异。

Method: 基于ack3发布的H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset，研究覆盖2026年1月1日至6月29日的135起事件及9.3986亿美元归因损失。研究识别其中68起事件的审计历史，并将攻击路径与事件发生前公开审计的范围进行比对，同时分析审计时间、损失时间分布和受影响项目类型；此外，通过排除两起超大额事件进行稳健性检验。

Result: 68起具有审计记录的事件中，46起攻击路径完全位于已识别的公开审计范围之外，20起至少部分位于某一审计范围内，2起无法判定。范围外路径占事件数量的67.6%，占报告损失的94.4%。排除两起最大事件后，该损失占比降至72.1%，但总体方向不变。

Conclusion: 项目存在审计历史不能证明具体攻击路径已获得审计覆盖。评估安全保障时，应同时报告审计对象、版本、时间和功能范围，并将项目级“已审计”标签与事件路径级覆盖情况明确区分。

Abstract: Smart-contract audits cover defined artifacts at a specific time, but the label audited is often treated as project-wide assurance. We analyze audit history and incident-path scope across 135 DeFi security incidents using the H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset published by cybersecurity company ack3 (https://ack3.ai), covering 1 January to 29 June 2026. The corpus reports USD 939.86 million in attributed loss. Audit history was identified for 68 incidents. Of these, 46 attack paths were outside all identified public pre-incident audit scopes, 20 were inside at least one scope, and 2 were unresolved. Within this 68-incident subset, outside-scope paths represented 67.6% by count and 94.4% of reported loss. The loss-weighted result was concentrated in two large incidents; excluding both reduced the share to 72.1%, while preserving the direction of the result. We also describe audit age, temporal loss distribution, and affected project types. The results show that project-level audit history and incident-path scope are distinct variables.

</details>

Abstract: This study examines user perceptions of mobile applications (apps) versus web browsers for accessing online services, with an emphasis on security, privacy, and usability aspects. Through a combination of an experiment and a survey with Android smartphone users, the research seeks to identify the key concerns and preferences that influence their choice between mobile apps and web browsers. The findings will offer valuable insights for developers to improve the security, privacy, and usability of both platforms by addressing user concerns and misconceptions.

</details>
