*Pol Henarejos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Vaulted Passkeys，一种支持设备绑定凭据可迁移恢复的架构：在源设备可用时导出受保护的凭据状态，并在未来硬件上恢复，同时避免复制完整认证器或向普通桌面软件暴露明文私钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恢复方案通常要求用户在设备失效前预先注册额外认证器，本质上是凭据冗余而非原始凭据备份，且要求替换硬件提前准备。本文旨在弥补设备更换、恢复与受控迁移中的可移植性缺口。

Method: 系统使用随机生成的256位 Kvault 保护经认证的 PKV1 凭据封装，通过 HKDF 派生密钥，并定义四种明确的 AEAD 保护配置。架构区分注册与导出/导入流程，将必需的 vault 与可选的身份信息分离。论文还给出角色分离的系统模型、线缆格式、威胁分析、实现映射及可证伪的评估方案。

Result: 原型验证了该架构的工程可行性：凭据状态能够在源设备可用时被保护性导出，并在后续获得的硬件上恢复。论文未宣称形式化安全证明，也未将该方案定位为最终标准。

Conclusion: Vaulted Passkeys 为设备绑定凭据提供了区别于预先注册备用认证器的恢复机制，在保持私钥不直接暴露和避免完整认证器克隆的同时支持受控迁移。其安全性与标准化适用性仍需通过后续实证评估和更严格分析确认。

Abstract: Hardware authenticators deliberately resist private-key extraction, yet replacement, disaster recovery, and controlled migration create a legitimate need for portability. Existing guidance for device-bound credentials commonly reduces recovery risk by registering an additional authenticator before failure. That creates an independent credential registration and requires replacement hardware to exist in advance; it is redundancy, not a backup of the original credential. This paper addresses the resulting recovery gap by exporting protected credential state while the source is available and restoring it to hardware acquired later, without cloning a complete authenticator or exposing plaintext private keys to routine desktop software. We propose Vaulted Passkeys, a device-bound architecture in which a random 256-bit Kvault protects authenticated PKV1 credential envelopes through HKDF-separated keys and four explicit AEAD profiles. The design separates enrollment from export/import and the required vault from optional identity. We contribute a role-separated system model, wire format, threat analysis, implementation mapping, and falsifiable evaluation plan. The prototype demonstrates feasibility but is neither a formal security proof nor a proposed final standard.

</details>


### [25] [TLF: Rapid Characterization of RF Transceiver Parameters in Embedded Systems via Bus-Level Interception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13815)
*Larry Hernandez,Sergey Bratus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: We present TLF (Transceiver Lifter Framework), a tool for recovering RF transceiver configuration and runtime behavior from bus-level traces captured between a microcontroller and its transceiver IC. A stateful protocol decoder, built against the transceiver's register and data interface, reconstructs operating RF parameters and behavior from intercepted register writes and FIFO transfers. For bus-attached transceivers whose hardware-cryptography keys are loaded through the intercepted host interface, key material is also recoverable. Where the firmware drives frequency hopping -- either through a hardware-assisted engine or a custom schedule -- the decoder extracts the channel table, hop sequence, and timing. We evaluate the approach on two targets from different Semtech families: an SX1233-based UAV C2 modem employing firmware-level FHSS with per-packet sync word rotation, and an SX1276-based Meshtastic node exercising the LoRa register overlay. From a single bus capture, processed in seconds, TLF recovers the complete register-exposed RF configuration (modulation, band plan, phase behavior) without prior knowledge of the target firmware -- sufficient to configure a matched receiver or develop targeted countermeasures. Above the chip layer, a pluggable protocol decoder interprets recovered FIFO payloads as application PDUs, demonstrated end-to-end on Meshtastic. Firmware-level cryptographic state remains, as expected, opaque. The approach requires physical access or emulation of the target hardware, and its recovery depth is bounded by the transceiver's register interface: parameters implemented entirely in firmware (custom FEC, whitening, encryption) are observable only as opaque FIFO payloads.

</details>


### [26] [Verified Pythagorean Composition for Adaptive Cryptographic Games: Noise Flooding in Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13846)
*Yi Lee,Alexandru Cojocaru,Junyi Liu,Xiaodi Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文使用Rocq与SSProve形式化验证噪声淹没防御的安全性证明，针对自适应解密查询避免传统混合论证的线性损失，并得到关键的平方根损失界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声淹没是近似同态加密中抵御解密攻击的标准方法，但其安全证明对查询组合十分敏感。逐次将每个解密回答替换为统计接近的模拟会产生关于查询次数q的线性损失，难以满足参数要求，因此需要更精细的组合分析。

Method: 在任意满足近似正确性和IND-CPA安全性的全同态加密方案上，形式化任意q查询IND-CPAD对手的安全归约。方法以条件KL散度成本为核心，通过新的关系程序逻辑在SSProve语义中组合这些成本；其Pythagorean判断避免在中间步骤转换为统计距离，并利用经验证的轨迹编译器将局部预言机规则提升到任意自适应程序，最后仅进行一次统计距离转换。

Result: 证明了不等式：Pr[IND-CPAD_NF^A=1] ≤ β_CPA(B_{A,q}) + √(qn)/(2γ)。其中n为明文维度，γ为噪声淹没宽度乘数，安全损失关于查询数q呈平方根增长，而非传统混合论证导致的线性增长。

Conclusion: 该工作机器验证了噪声淹没安全证明中的关键KL散度组合论证，说明Rocq与SSProve能够可靠处理自适应解密查询和单次最终转换，并为近似同态加密防御的形式化安全分析提供了可复用的程序逻辑与验证框架。

Abstract: Noise flooding is a standard defense against decryption attacks on approximate homomorphic encryption, but its security proof is unusually sensitive to composition. Replacing each of $q$ adaptive decryption answers with a statistically close simulation and applying an ordinary hybrid argument loses linearly in $q$. The cryptographic proof instead accumulates conditional Kullback-Leibler (KL) costs and converts to statistical distance once, giving the parameter-critical square-root loss.
  We machine-check this argument using Rocq and SSProve. Given any fully homomorphic encryption scheme that is approximately correct and IND-CPA secure, we formalize a reduction for every $q$-query IND-CPAD adversary and prove \[
  \Pr[\mathsf{IND\text{-}CPAD}_{\mathsf{NF}}^{\mathcal A}=1]
  \leq β_{\mathsf{CPA}}(\mathcal B_{\mathcal A,q})
  + \frac{\sqrt{qn}}{2γ}. \] where $n$ is the plaintext dimension and $γ$ is the flooding-width multiplier. Our proof constructs a new relational program logic over SSProve semantics. Its Pythagorean judgment composes conditional KL budgets without converting them to statistical distance, and a verified trace compiler lifts a local oracle rule to arbitrary adaptive programs with a single final conversion.

</details>


### [27] [CipherSight: Robust Website Fingerprinting via Record-Resource Semantic Supervision under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13905)
*Runhan Song,Qiqi Liu,Chuanzhou Pan,Zhenquan Ding,Youquan Xian,Chongru Fan,Lei Cui,Wei Wang,Zhiyu Hao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CipherSight是一种基于TLS记录的分层式HTTPS网站指纹识别框架，通过联合建模记录属性、流内依赖和流间交互，提升模型在时间漂移、地理漂移及大规模网站分类场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖原始TCP数据包序列，容易受到传输层伪影影响，难以学习稳定且可泛化的网站表示。在真实部署中，时间和地理变化会造成显著的分布外问题，开放世界中未见网站也进一步降低识别性能。

Method: CipherSight以TLS记录为基本建模单元，联合编码多种记录级属性；采用分层架构，分别捕获同一流内TLS记录之间的依赖关系，以及并发流之间的交互模式。训练过程中引入掩码记录建模任务以学习上下文流量语义，并利用细粒度记录—资源标注作为特权监督，通过结构感知目标和语义蒸馏增强表示学习。

Result: 在包含超过2000个网站类别的闭世界实验中，CipherSight达到95.41%的准确率；在时间漂移和地理漂移场景下均保持超过90%的准确率，并持续优于所有评估基线。

Conclusion: 基于TLS记录的层次化建模能够减少对TCP传输层细节的依赖，并有效利用流内结构、跨流关系及辅助语义监督，从而显著提升HTTPS网站指纹识别在实际分布变化下的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: HTTPS website fingerprinting (WF) aims to identify visited websites from metadata observable in encrypted traffic. However, real-world deployments introduce a significant out-of-distribution (OOD) problem caused by temporal and geographic changes, while previously unseen websites are common in open-world scenarios. Existing methods primarily learn from raw TCP packet sequences and struggle to capture stable and generalizable website representations, resulting in performance degradation under practical conditions.
  We propose CipherSight, a TLS-record-based hierarchical framework for robust HTTPS WF. Unlike existing approaches that rely on TCP packet sequences and are sensitive to transport-layer artifacts, CipherSight learns website representations from TLS records by jointly encoding multiple record-level attributes. It introduces a hierarchical architecture that captures both intra-flow dependencies among TLS records and inter-flow interactions across concurrent flows, enabling the model to exploit structural patterns in HTTPS traffic. Besides, to learn robust representations, CipherSight employs a masked record modeling (MRM) task to capture contextual traffic semantics and leverages fine-grained record-resource annotations as privileged supervision through structure-aware objectives and semantic distillation. Experiments show that CipherSight achieves 95.41% accuracy across more than 2,000 website classes in the closed-world setting and maintains over 90% accuracy under both temporal and geographic drift, consistently outperforming all evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [28] [Characterizing the Variance Envelope: A Multi-Dimensional Analysis of Spectre Telemetry Across Architectures and Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13920)
*Jaya Keshava Chandra Kotha,Jean-Luc Gaudiot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究不同处理器架构、攻击变体、执行节奏和背景噪声对硬件性能计数器（HPC）攻击特征的影响，指出静态机器学习检测模型在真实环境中可靠性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于HPC和机器学习的检测方法多在受控环境下验证，难以应对系统噪声、攻击变体多样性及流量节奏变化，因此需要系统刻画攻击特征的变化范围。

Method: 在Intel、ARM和AMD三类架构上构建实验矩阵，覆盖三种攻击变体、四种执行节奏模式和四种背景噪声条件，比较不同环境下HPC指标的变化，并分析Prime+Probe攻击在不同微架构上的表现。

Result: 实验表明，攻击相关HPC特征具有高度脆弱性，会受到执行环境显著扰动，导致静态检测模型的泛化能力下降。此外，Prime+Probe在AMD Jaguar上持续失效，反映出由硬件微架构导致的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 可靠的运行时检测不能依赖固定模型，而应采用面向处理器架构的自适应监控机制，并显式建模攻击特征在不同环境中的变化范围。

Abstract: Hardware attacks like Spectre exploit built-in processor vulnerabilities, leaving anomalous footprints in Hardware Performance Counter (HPC) metrics. While machine learning can detect these footprints in controlled settings, static models fail in the real world when confronted with background system noise, diverse attack variants, and adversarial traffic pacing. To close this gap, this paper characterizes the "variance envelope"-the full range of how attack signatures shift- across Intel, ARM, and AMD architectures. We evaluate an extensive experimental matrix encompassing three attack variants, four pacing modes, and four background-noise conditions. Our analysis proves that HPC signatures are highly fragile and easily warped by their execution environment. Furthermore, we expose a critical microarchitectural bottleneck: the persistent, hardware-level failure of Prime+Probe attacks on the AMD Jaguar. Ultimately, this comprehensive characterization demonstrates that reliable runtime detection requires architecture-aware, adaptive monitoring rather than static models.

</details>


### [29] [CoSA: Context-Aware Severity Assessment via Context Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13928)
*Jinfeng Jiang,Yikun Li,Chengran Yang,Ting Zhang,Wen Bin Leow,Yide Yin,Eng Lieh Ouh,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSA，一种面向代码仓库的上下文感知漏洞严重性评估方法，通过检索与压缩仓库证据来预测CVSS基础指标。实验表明，其准确率和Macro-F1分别较最佳基线提升14.4%和15.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工评估CVSS基础指标成本高；现有自动化方法通常仅利用函数级信息或直接依赖大语言模型，难以捕获分散在整个代码仓库中的有效证据，并容易受到大量无关信息干扰。

Method: CoSA首先构建代码属性图，并采用两阶段仓库剪枝：通过轻量级静态分析保留结构上相近的上下文，再利用智能体式大语言模型筛选与CVSS相关的上下文并收集支持性证据。随后，大语言模型将检索结果整理为按CVSS指标划分的紧凑文本摘要，并输入轻量级Transformer预测器。研究还构建了包含6816个带CVSS标签实例、覆盖90种CWE类型的仓库级数据集。

Result: 在真实世界漏洞数据上的实验显示，CoSA稳定优于函数级方法和纯大语言模型基线；相较表现最佳的基线，预测准确率提升14.4%，Macro-F1提升15.3%。

Conclusion: 显式的、面向CVSS指标的仓库级上下文检索能够有效补充漏洞严重性评估所需证据，提升自动化预测的准确性与可靠性。

Abstract: Accurate vulnerability severity assessment is essential for prioritizing remediation, yet manually assessing Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS) base metrics remains labor-intensive. Existing automated approaches often fail to capture the repository-level evidence required for assessing many CVSS base metrics. Such repository-aware assessment is challenging because relevant evidence is scattered across the entire repository under heavy noise.
  To address these challenges, we present CoSA, a Context-aware vulnerability Severity Assessment approach that infers CVSS base metrics from repository artifacts. CoSA constructs a code property graph (CPG) and applies a two-stage repository-pruning strategy: lightweight static pruning to preserve structurally proximal context, followed by an agentic large language model (LLM)-guided pruning step to retain CVSS-relevant context while collecting supporting evidence. The LLM then consolidates the retrieved repository context into compact, CVSS metric-wise textual summaries, which are fed into a lightweight transformer predictor. We also construct a higher-quality repository-level dataset comprising 6,816 CVSS labeled instances spanning 90 Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) types.
  Experiments on real-world vulnerabilities show that CoSA consistently outperforms function-level and pure-LLM baselines. It improves prediction accuracy by 14.4% and Macro-F1 by 15.3% over the best-performing baseline, suggesting that explicit, metric-oriented repository context retrieval is crucial for practical and reliable automated severity assessment.

</details>


### [30] [Extracting and Verifying Illicit Bitcoin Addresses from Underground Forum Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13930)
*Abdoul Nasser Hassane Amadou,Arnaud Legout,Imane Fouad,Konstantin Avrachenkov,Anas Motii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复现的比特币非法活动地址标注流程，结合地下论坛证据、LLM筛选、专家复核与链上验证，构建了包含2,438个经人工确认地址的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有比特币标注数据集多依赖社区举报、区块链启发式方法、特定事件收集或专有流程，构建过程缺乏公开可复现性，且通常难以证明地址与非法活动存在直接关联。

Method: 从HackForums十五年历史存档中提取与比特币交易相关的讨论，使用大语言模型进行初筛，再由专家人工审核，并通过区块链数据验证地址与讨论中所述交易的关联。最终为地址保留论坛上下文证据、链上验证结果、时间信息及LLM筛选得到的犯罪类别。

Result: 数据集包含2,438个经人工核验的非法比特币地址，时间跨度为2010年至2024年，覆盖12类网络犯罪活动。研究同时发布时间元数据和完整的数据提取流程。

Conclusion: 该工作提高了非法比特币地址标注的证据性、透明度与可复现性，可为加密货币相关网络犯罪研究、地址识别和后续实证分析提供公开基准数据。

Abstract: Existing labeled Bitcoin datasets are largely derived from community-reported abuse, blockchain heuristics, incident-specific collections, or proprietary labeling processes. Their construction methods are rarely publicly reproducible and often provide limited evidence that an address was directly involved in illicit activity. We present a reproducible pipeline for constructing evidence-backed Bitcoin labels from HackForums, an underground cybercrime forum with fifteen years of archived activity. The pipeline combines LLM-assisted screening, expert review, and on-chain validation to identify Bitcoin addresses explicitly associated with illicit transactions discussed on the forum. Each released label is supported by contextual evidence from underground discussions and validated on-chain. The resulting dataset contains 2,438 manually verified illicit Bitcoin addresses spanning 2010-2024 and twelve cybercrime categories assigned during LLM screening. We release the dataset, temporal metadata, and the complete extraction pipeline to support reproducible research on cryptocurrency-facilitated cybercrime.

</details>


### [31] [Vectorized SQIsign Implementation Using AVX-512](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13948)
*Weize Wang,Chutong Wang,Yu Wu,Qifan Xue,Jieyu Zheng,Yunlei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于AVX-512 IFMA的SQIsign全面向量化实现，通过重构有限域运算、椭圆曲线运算、配对计算和同源映射，在结合Qlapoti技术后显著提升签名与验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQIsign具有紧凑的公钥和签名，但签名过程计算开销较高，限制了其实用部署。现有研究对SIMD向量化在同源密码中的优化潜力挖掘不足，因此本文旨在通过硬件级并行提升SQIsign效率。

Method: 基于AVX-512整数融合乘加（IFMA）指令集，对SQIsign计算栈进行系统性向量化重构，包括素域与扩域算术、椭圆曲线点运算、批量点倍乘、标量乘法，以及采用立方算术和二维同源求值的配对计算。同时结合Qlapoti技术进行整体性能优化，并讨论相关策略在后续AVX10架构及其他同源密码构造中的适用性。

Result: 在NIST安全等级I下，结合Qlapoti后，实现的签名速度达到参考实现的2.69倍，验证速度达到3.18倍。结果表明，AVX-512 IFMA能够有效降低SQIsign核心运算的执行开销。

Conclusion: SIMD向量化是提升同源后量子签名方案性能的重要且尚未充分研究的方向。本文验证了AVX-512 IFMA优化SQIsign的有效性，并提供了具有架构迁移潜力的方法基础，但摘要未报告代码开源情况、不同平台的完整基准结果及额外内存开销。

Abstract: SQIsign is the sole isogeny-based digital signature scheme submitted to the NIST Post-Quantum Cryptography standardization process, distinguished by its foundation on the hardness of the endomorphism ring problem for supersingular elliptic curves. While offering compact key and signature sizes, SQIsign's practical deployment is hindered by computationally intensive signing procedures. This paper presents the first comprehensive vectorized implementation of SQIsign utilizing the AVX-512 Integer Fused Multiply-Add (IFMA) instruction set architecture. By systematically redesigning the computational stack---encompassing prime-field and extension-field arithmetic, elliptic curve operations including batched point doubling and scalar multiplication, as well as pairing computations via cubical arithmetic and two-dimensional isogeny evaluations---we achieve substantial performance improvements over the reference implementation. When combined with Qlapoti technology, our implementation attains a $2.69\times$ speedup for signing and a $3.18\times$ improvement for verification at NIST security level I.
  Contrary to misconceptions regarding the obsolescence of AVX-512, we emphasize that Intel's AVX10 instruction set architecture (revision 10.2, scheduled for widespread deployment in late 2026) will standardize AVX-512 capabilities---including IFMA instructions---across both performance and efficiency cores, ensuring long-term viability of these optimization techniques. Furthermore, our vectorization strategies are architecture-agnostic and provide a methodological foundation applicable to broader isogeny-based cryptographic constructions. This work demonstrates that SIMD vectorization represents a critical yet underexplored optimization dimension for post-quantum isogeny-based schemes, independent of recent algorithmic advances.

</details>


### [32] [Structural Leakage in Graph Encryption: Attacks and Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13981)
*Hua Shen,Renzhi Chen,Ge Wu,Willy Susilo,Jing Chen,Mingwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析面向单点对最短路径查询的图加密方案结构泄漏问题，发现PathGES存在严重的重链—轻链分解不均衡，易遭受查询恢复和路径长度推断攻击；提出的BlindGES通过合并与划分机制及两级多重映射索引显著增强安全性并降低系统开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图加密方案支持图数据安全外包和高效查询，但其索引结构与编码方式可能泄漏查询内容、路径长度等敏感信息。本文旨在系统评估PathGES的结构泄漏风险，并设计兼顾安全性与效率的改进方案。

Method: 首先分析PathGES的重链—轻链分解和规范片段编码，评估令牌—路径映射的不均衡性及其泄漏影响；随后提出Fragment Tree攻击以利用结构特征恢复查询内容，并结合Falzon-Paterson攻击和侧信道方法进行实验；最后设计BlindGES，引入Merge-and-Divide机制与两级多重映射索引，并通过安全性证明、复杂度分析和七个真实数据集进行评估。

Result: 在真实数据集上，PathGES中超过99%的令牌—路径映射为一对一映射，导致查询恢复和路径长度推断风险；Fragment Tree攻击在稀疏图上的查询精确恢复率最高达到10.24%。BlindGES将一对一映射比例降至20%以下，使路径长度泄漏低于1%，同时将初始化时间降低50%，存储开销降低32%。

Conclusion: PathGES的结构设计难以有效隐藏查询和路径信息，其安全性会受到分解不均衡与索引可辨识性的显著影响。BlindGES通过提高映射歧义性和改进索引组织，在降低泄漏风险的同时改善了系统效率，体现出更好的安全—性能折中。

Abstract: Graph encryption schemes (GES) enable secure outsourcing of graph data while supporting efficient queries. This report provides a comprehensive analysis of structural leakage in GES for single-pair shortest path (SPSP) queries, integrating findings from two recent works. First, we analyze PathGES, a scheme designed to resist query recovery attacks through heavy-light decomposition (HLD) and canonical fragment encoding. Our analysis reveals that PathGES suffers from significant imbalances in HLD decomposition, with over 99% of token-path mappings being one-to-one on real-world datasets, enabling both the Falzon-Paterson attack and side-channel inference of path lengths. Second, we present Fragment Tree attack that exploits these structural weaknesses to recover query contents, achieving up to 10.24% exact recovery on sparse graphs. Third, we introduce BlindGES, an enhanced scheme incorporating a Merge-and-Divide mechanism and two-level multimap index that reduces one-to-one mappings to below 20%, cuts setup time by 50%, reduces storage overhead by 32%, and limits path length leakage to under 1%. This report systematically presents attack methodologies, defense mechanisms, security proofs, and experimental evaluations on seven real-world datasets.

</details>


### [33] [P2Skill: Privacy Preserving Skill Distillation for Cloud-Local LLM Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14094)
*Myunghoon Ryu,Geunpyo Park,Sungjoon Lee,XinYu Piao,Jong-Kook Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: P2Skill是一种无需隐私专项微调的提示驱动技能蒸馏方法，通过本地小模型完成任务分解、PII识别与路由、改写和结果重构，在保护用户隐私的同时提升云端大模型推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端大模型具有较强推理能力，但直接发送请求可能泄露个人可识别信息。现有提示扰动、实体掩码和模型微调方法可能破坏上下文语义，或需要额外训练，因此需要一种无需专门训练且能保持任务语义的本地隐私保护方案。

Method: P2Skill将隐私保护流程封装为可执行技能提示，由本地小语言模型自主完成任务分解、PII感知路由、语义改写和答案重构。云端大模型根据执行失败案例迭代优化技能提示，使本地模型能够超越对固定PII模式的记忆，并且不依赖隐私专用微调或额外检测器。

Result: 在涵盖四个领域的基准测试中，P2Skill相较既有基线分别取得1.69倍和3.66倍更高的隐私保护推理质量，表明其在隐私保护与推理效果之间具有较好的综合性能。

Conclusion: P2Skill证明了基于提示的技能蒸馏可以让本地小模型承担隐私感知的请求预处理与结果重构，在无需额外训练的情况下提升云本地协同推理的隐私保护质量与泛化能力。

Abstract: Cloud-local LLM inference systems have the potential to use the reasoning capability of large cloud models while protecting sensitive user data on personal devices. Cloud-bound requests must exclude personally identifiable information (PII) to prevent external data leakage. Existing privacy-preserving methods rely on prompt perturbation, entity masking, or model fine-tuning, but these approaches may distort contextual semantics or require additional training. This paper proposes P2Skill, a prompt-based skill distillation method in which a local small language model (SLM) autonomously performs decomposition, PII-aware routing, paraphrasing, and reconstruction by following the skill prompts. Skills are iteratively refined from execution failures by a cloud LLM, enabling the local SLM to generalize beyond memorized PII patterns, and therefore P2Skill requires no privacy-specific fine-tuning or learned auxiliary detectors. Evaluation on a four-domain benchmark shows that P2Skill achieves $1.69\times$ and $3.66\times$ higher privacy-preserved inference quality than previous baselines.

</details>


### [34] [BGA: A noise-immune neural distillation framework for malicious signature extraction in high-entropy encrypted flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14126)
*Sheng Hong,Yixuan Huang,Weiwei Jiang,Junyuan Zhang,Jiacheng Wang,Ruijian Jiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BGA加密流量威胁检测框架，通过特征筛选、WGAN-GP数据增强、BiLSTM与自适应门控多头注意力，提升高噪声和类别不均衡场景下的检测性能。实验显示关键指标超过95.2%，相较普通Transformer具有更强抗噪性，并具备较低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLS 1.3加密流量中的随机密码学噪声会导致注意力稀释，同时稀有类别样本不足会降低少数类检测召回率。因此，研究旨在分离高判别性控制面特征与噪声，并增强对少数类异常状态命令注入的识别能力。

Method: 首先使用ANOVA筛选并解耦高判别性工业设定点特征与随机加密噪声；随后引入满足1-Lipschitz约束的WGAN-GP生成少数类样本，以缓解类别不均衡。模型主体结合BiLSTM提取时序依赖，并利用自适应门控多头注意力动态抑制加密伪影、强化异常模式。研究在CIC-IDS-2018和Edge-IIoT数据集上进行评估，并通过噪声注入测试验证鲁棒性，同时测量推理延迟。

Result: WGAN-GP使少数类异常状态命令注入的检测召回率提升43.2%；整体关键指标超过95.2%。噪声压力测试中，BGA相比普通Transformer保持8.57%的性能优势。实测推理延迟为0.2820毫秒，按理论ARM缩放估计为1.6920毫秒，显示出边缘实时部署潜力。摘要未明确说明95.2%对应的具体指标、基线配置、统计显著性及跨数据集泛化细节。

Conclusion: BGA通过特征解耦、生成式少数类增强和门控注意力协同提升了加密流量检测的精度、少数类召回率与抗噪能力，并兼顾较低延迟。该方法具有工业边缘网关部署前景，但仍需通过消融实验、真实硬件测试、跨场景验证和更完整的评估指标证明其工程可靠性。

Abstract: To mitigate attention dilution in high-entropy TLS 1.3 flows, we propose BGA, a noise-immune neural distillation framework for encrypted threat intelligence.The methodology first employs Analysis of Variance (ANOVA) to decouple high-discriminatory control-plane features - specifically industrial setpoints - from stochastic cryptographic noise. To resolve the extreme class imbalance within a corpus of 86,878 flow records, a Wasserstein GAN with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP) module, enforcing the 1-Lipschitz constraint, is integrated to synthesize high-fidelity minority samples, elevating the detection recall of rare Malicious State Command Injections(MSCI) attacks by 43.2%. At its core, the BGA architecture integrates Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) for temporal dependency extraction and an Adaptive Gated Multi-Head Attention mechanism. This gated unit functions as a neural filter to dynamically suppress encryption artifacts while amplifying malicious signatures. Extensive evaluations on CIC-IDS-2018 and Edge-IIoT benchmarks demonstrate a performance ceiling exceeding 95.2% across all key metrics. Furthermore, noise-injection stress tests confirm BGAs superior structural resilience with a 8.57% performance margin over vanilla Transformers, while its ultra-low inference latency of 0.2820 ms (estimated 1.6920 ms via theoretical scaling for ARM) indicates a high potential for real-time feasibility on heterogeneous industrial edge gateways, providing a promising architectural baseline for future hardware implementation.

</details>


### [35] [A Hybrid LLM-Based Framework for Automated Security Annotation Generation in Business Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14370)
*Md Kamrul Islam,Tiphaine Henry,Mattia Salnitri,Julius Köpke,Sami Souihi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合式自动化框架，从BPMN流程模型和安全需求文档中生成符合SecBPMN2规范的安全标注。实验表明，该方法在结构有效性、精确率、效率和一致性方面优于或接近人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全业务流程建模需要将安全要求准确映射为流程标注，但从自然语言需求中人工提取和标注通常依赖专家，成本高、耗时长且容易出现遗漏、错误或位置不当的问题。因此，需要一种可扩展、可验证的自动化方法。

Method: 框架以BPMN流程模型和安全需求文档为输入，结合大语言模型进行语义提取，再通过模式约束映射、基于规则的规范化处理和确定性验证生成SecBPMN2安全标注。研究使用来自不同领域的27个流程模型构成数据集，并将系统结果与人工安全分析人员的标注结果进行比较。

Result: 系统能够持续生成结构有效且模式完整度较高的SecBPMN2标注。其精确率为0.58，高于人工分析人员的0.29；召回率为0.52，与人工结果的0.50相近；错误或位置不当的标注减少近50%。此外，自动生成速度显著快于人工标注。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型的语义理解能力与规则、模式约束及确定性验证相结合，可以有效降低安全流程建模工作量，并提升标注的一致性、可靠性和可扩展性，为面向安全设计的业务流程建模提供了可行基础。

Abstract: The modelling and analysis of secure business processes require the incorporation of security annotations into process models. Although BPMN extensions, including SecBPMN2, exist for this purpose, the derivation of accurate and complete security annotations from natural-language specifications remains a manual, expert-intensive, and error-prone task. This paper presents a hybrid framework that takes a BPMN process model and a security requirements document as input and automatically generates security annotations adhering to the SecBPMN2 specification. The approach combines Large Language Model (LLM)--based semantic extraction with schema-constrained mapping, rule-based normalization, and deterministic validation. The framework is evaluated comprehensively on a curated dataset of 27 process models from various domains. The results indicate that it consistently produces structurally valid SecBPMN2 annotations with high schema completeness. Compared to human security analysts, the system achieves substantially higher precision (0.58 vs. 0.29) while maintaining comparable recall (0.52 vs. 0.50) and reduces erroneous or misplaced annotations by nearly 50%. In addition, annotation generation is significantly faster than manual annotation. These findings demonstrate that hybrid LLM- and rule-based automation can reduce modeling effort while improving consistency and reliability, thereby providing a scalable foundation for security-by-design BPM.

</details>


### [36] [MazeRunner: Nonlinear Task and Clue Orchestration for LLM-driven Black-Box Automated Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14216)
*Zhenyuan Li,Yi Jiang,Junjie Cheng,Yaokun Li,Jing Qiu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MazeRunner是一种面向真实黑盒渗透测试的自主智能体系统，通过任务与线索协同机制处理非线性攻击路径、失败诊断和长期证据关联，在HTB目标上的完成率及权限获取能力均优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的渗透测试智能体主要适用于线性、简化流程，容易陷入深度优先探索，误判失败原因，并遗忘较早阶段的重要线索。真实黑盒测试需要根据环境反馈持续推断未知攻击图、切换分支并恢复前置条件。

Method: MazeRunner采用三智能体任务与线索编排框架，分别负责全局任务编排、上下文密集型执行和面向失败的复盘。系统持续维护任务状态与环境证据，从而支持动作修订、前置条件恢复、攻击分支切换以及跨长时间跨度的线索关联。实验在10个近期发布的HTB目标上进行，每次系统—目标运行限制为2000万LLM令牌，并避免泄露目标特定解法。

Result: 使用Claude Sonnet 4.5时，MazeRunner完成47.7%的标注子任务，高于PentestGPT-V2的36.2%和Claude Code的34.2%。MazeRunner在6个目标上获得用户级或更高权限，其中2个目标获得最高权限；相同模型的基线方法仅分别在2个目标上获得用户级权限，且均未获得最高权限。执行轨迹分析表明，MazeRunner探索了更多攻击分支，并更高效地获取交互式命令环境。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离全局编排、上下文执行与失败复盘，并持久化管理任务和证据，MazeRunner增强了LLM智能体在非线性黑盒渗透测试中的探索、纠错和长期推理能力。结果显示，该架构较现有基线具有更强的子任务完成能力、权限提升能力和分支探索效率。

Abstract: Penetration testing is essential yet resource-intensive. Although large language models (LLMs) show promise for automating security auditing, existing agents mainly execute end-to-end workflows in simplified linear scenarios. Real-world black-box testing is fundamentally nonlinear: the attack graph is initially unknown and must be incrementally inferred from environmental feedback. Observations may reveal multiple attack branches, failures are often ambiguous, and critical clues may span long action horizons. Existing agents therefore tend to become trapped in depth-first exploration, misdiagnose failures, and forget prior evidence. We present MazeRunner, an autonomous penetration testing system built on a three-agent task-and-clue orchestration framework. It separates global orchestration, context-intensive execution, and failure-oriented review while persistently maintaining task states and environmental evidence. This design supports action revision, prerequisite recovery, branch switching, and long-range clue correlation. We evaluate MazeRunner on 10 recently released HTB targets, limiting each system-target run to 20 million LLM tokens and preventing target-specific solution leakage. With Claude Sonnet 4.5, MazeRunner completes 47.7% of annotated subtasks, compared with 36.2% for PentestGPT-V2 and 34.2% for Claude Code. It achieves user-level or higher access on six targets, including root access on two; each same-model baseline reaches user-level access on only two targets and never obtains root access. Execution-trace analysis further shows that MazeRunner explores more attack branches and acquires shells more efficiently.

</details>


### [37] [A Four-Axis Trustworthiness Benchmark for LLM-as-Judge in Principle-Based Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14329)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出并发布Principle-Bench，用于系统评估原则导向监管中的LLM裁判。核心结论是：仅报告总体准确率不足；部署级系统必须同时报告准确率、释义鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性和校准度，尤其应按监管原则披露对抗欺骗风险与事后校准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原则导向监管依赖“公平、清晰、不误导”“实现良好结果”等评价性标准，难以化约为简单的二元规则。LLM正被用于替代人工判断，但现有评估通常偏重正常输入上的准确率，忽视措辞改写、关键词堆砌等操纵，以及模型置信度是否可信，因而可能高估其监管适用性。

Method: 作者构建Principle-Bench，包含168个加密资产金融推广情景，并映射至两项英国金融行为监管原则。基准依据预注册标注规范，提供三类干预样本：释义改写、对抗性关键词堆砌和决策边界扰动；据此从准确率、释义鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性及校准度四个维度评测。论文还提出Ceca，即校准的示例簇评估方法，可输出逐示例的精确反事实归因，以增强审计性。比较对象包括关键词计数、三种句向量嵌入器、开源权重LLM裁判与校准级联系统。

Result: 没有任何方法能在四项指标上全面领先。一个120B参数LLM裁判在良性输入中表现最强，但在消费者义务相关的关键词堆砌输入上，准确率由0.74降至0.27，下降47个百分点，显示其容易受到“合规表演式”文本操纵。来自不同模型家族的第二个裁判在该划分上的一致性仅为Cohen's kappa=0.16，表明问题更可能源自模型行为而非基准语料本身。

Conclusion: 原则导向监管的LLM裁判不能以单一总体准确率作为可靠性依据。实际部署应采用覆盖四轴的评估框架，按具体原则报告对抗样本下的受骗程度，并提供事后校准信息；同时，Ceca所提供的反事实归因可为模型判断提供更强的可审计性与诊断能力。

Abstract: Principle-based regulation, with evaluative standards such as "fair, clear, and not misleading" or "deliver good outcomes", cannot be reduced to binary predicates, and LLM-as-judge is increasingly used as the substitute. Our position is that any such judge must be evaluated on four axes: accuracy, paraphrase robustness, adversarial robustness, and calibration. We release Principle-Bench, 168 cryptoasset financial-promotion scenarios mapped to two UK FCA principles, with paraphrase, adversarial keyword-stuffing, and boundary perturbations authored under a pre-registered rubric; the first benchmark covering all four axes for principle-based regulation. We also introduce Ceca (Calibrated Exemplar-Cluster Assessment): a calibrated, auditable assessor that emits exact per-exemplar counterfactual attributions. Across keyword counting, three sentence-transformer embedders, an open-weight LLM-judge, and a calibrated cascade, no method dominates all four axes. A 120B LLM-judge, strongest on benign inputs, loses 47 accuracy points (0.74 to 0.27) on keyword-stuffed Consumer Duty inputs: "compliance theatre." A second judge from a different model family agrees only at Cohen's kappa = 0.16 on that split, localising the failure to the model rather than the corpus. Any deployment-grade LLM-judge for principle-based regulation must report per-principle adversarial deception and post-hoc calibration alongside aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [38] [STINER: Automated Extraction of Strategic Cyber Threat Intelligence from X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14418)
*Yasir Ech-Chammakhy,Oussama Azrara,Jaafar Chbili,Anas Motii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出STINER，一个面向社交媒体战略网络威胁情报抽取的实体识别体系，包含专家标注语料库、八类细粒度实体 taxonomy 及多模型基准。DarkBERT取得89.33%的严格F1，并支持对欧洲威胁态势和早期攻击信号进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体能够比正式厂商报告更早发布数据泄露和攻击信息，但其非正式、噪声高且表达不规则的语言使传统NER模型难以准确识别威胁主体、行业和地点等战略实体，限制了自动化威胁研判能力。

Method: 作者构建了包含2100条真实威胁告警的专家标注数据集，并设计包含Threat Actor、Sector和Location等战略实体在内的八类分类体系。研究比较了9个模型、12种配置，覆盖通用编码器、领域适配编码器、开放模式抽取模型，以及零样本和微调条件下的生成式大语言模型；随后使用STINER-DarkBERT开展2025年上半年欧洲威胁态势分析。

Result: DarkBERT达到89.33%的严格F1，优于通用基线和微调大语言模型；后者还具有明显更高的推理延迟。基于抽取结果的欧洲分析总体符合官方对主要目标的报告，同时揭示了西班牙攻击活动具有不同的可见性特征，并在厂商报告回顾性描述之前识别出SafePay勒索软件活动的早期信号。

Conclusion: STINER为社交媒体战略网络威胁情报抽取提供了专门的分类体系、数据资源和系统化评测框架。领域适配编码器在准确性与推理效率之间表现出更优的实用性，可用于从实时社交媒体信息中提取早期、可操作的威胁态势信号。

Abstract: Strategic Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) focuses on high-level insights, such as identifying targeted industries, attributing attacks to specific ransomware groups, and assessing the scale of data loss. Today, X (formerly Twitter) has become the fastest source for this intelligence, often hosting real-time breach announcements days before formal vendor reports. Converting this raw chatter into actionable intelligence requires navigating a complex linguistic landscape. Conventional Named Entity Recognition (NER) models struggle to parse the informal and highly irregular dialect of social media, creating a blind spot for automated defense systems. To address this challenge, we introduce STINER, a taxonomy and expert-annotated corpus for extracting strategic intelligence from social media streams. We construct a high-quality, expert-annotated dataset of 2,100 real-world alerts and propose a granular taxonomy of eight entity types centered on strategic pivots such as Threat Actor, Sector, and Location. We benchmark nine models across 12 evaluated configurations, spanning general-purpose and domain-adapted encoders, open-schema extraction, and generative LLMs in both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings. Domain-adapted encoders such as DarkBERT reach a strict F1-score of 89.33%, outperforming both general-purpose baselines and fine-tuned Large Language Models, which additionally incur substantially higher inference latency. Leveraging STINER-DarkBERT, we conduct a European threat landscape analysis for H1 2025. Our results align with official reporting on major targets while highlighting the distinct visibility profile of attacks in Spain, and illustrate how social-media-driven extraction can surface early signals of the SafePay ransomware campaign prior to its retrospective characterization in vendor threat landscape reports.

</details>


### [39] [Lower Bounds on Black-Box Constructions of Pseudorandom Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14501)
*Bar Alon,Itai Dinur,Muthuramakrishnan Venkitasubramaniam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究从伪随机生成器（PRG）黑盒构造伪随机函数（PRF）的调用复杂度，证明在完全黑盒模型下，非自适应PRG调用次数不能同时低于o(n/log n)和o(in/log in)，且即使对弱PRF、单比特输出及随机独立查询也成立；对足够长输出的弱PRF，即使允许自适应查询仍有下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GGM构造通常需要对PRG进行ω(log n)次调用，而此前仅对特定树型构造证明了其最优性。更基本的单次PRG调用问题尚未解决，因此需要理解完全黑盒PRF构造的普适性下界。

Method: 在完全黑盒模型中同时限制构造和安全性归约均为黑盒，并要求归约与对手交互次数不依赖于对手在每次交互中对底层函数的查询次数。作者针对非自适应PRG查询建立调用次数下界，并进一步分析弱PRF、单比特输出、均匀独立随机查询以及允许自适应PRG查询的情形。

Result: 对于输入长度为n的PRG和输入长度为in的PRF，任何满足上述模型要求的构造都不能使用o(n/log n)及o(in/log in)的非自适应PRG调用。该结论甚至适用于单比特输出的弱PRF，且对手只能进行独立同分布的均匀随机查询。此外，对于输出足够长的弱PRF，即使构造可自适应查询PRG，也存在相应的调用复杂度下界。

Conclusion: 研究首次对广泛的完全黑盒PRF构造给出显著的PRG调用下界，表明显著低于线性除以对数的调用复杂度通常不可能实现。结果将已知的树型构造下界推广到更一般的构造，并进一步覆盖弱PRF与自适应查询场景，但尚未解决单次PRG调用是否可能的问题。

Abstract: In their seminal work, Goldreich, Goldwasser, and Micali [CRYPTO 1984] constructed a pseudorandom function (PRF) using a black-box access to a pseudorandom generator (PRG). When combined with Levin's domain extension technique, the GGM construction invokes the PRG $ω(\log n)$ times, where $n$ denotes the input length to the PRG. To this day, no black-box construction achieving fewer calls is known.
  Recently, Beimel, Malkin, and Mazor [CRYPTO 2024] showed that for a certain family of constructions, which they termed \emph{tree constructions}, the GGM construction is optimal. However, the basic challenge of whether a PRF can be built with just \emph{one invocation} of the PRG still remains open.
  In this work, we consider fully black-box constructions of PRFs from PRGs, where both the construction and the reduction are required to be black-box, and the number of interactions the reduction makes with the adversary is independent of the number of oracle calls the adversary makes to its underlying function within each interaction. Our main result shows that no such construction can have $o(n/\log n)$ and $o(\mathsf{in}/\log\mathsf{in})$ \emph{non-adaptive} calls to the PRG, where $\mathsf{in}$ is the input length of the PRF. This impossibility holds even for weak PRFs with one-bit output, where the adversary is restricted to making i.i.d. uniformly random queries. In addition, we prove a lower bound for weak PRFs with sufficiently long outputs that holds even when the construction is allowed to make adaptive queries to the PRG.

</details>

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析NASA Core Flight Software（cFS）中的内部信任边界，发现其模块间共享权限和缺乏细粒度隔离，使单个受损组件能够以合法身份实施难以区分的越权行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着航天器软件化和互联程度提高，机载飞行软件已成为关键安全边界。然而，主流架构常将组件视为相互信任的对等实体，虽然便于集成，却削弱了内部隔离、访问控制和行为审计能力。

Method: 研究以NASA cFS为对象，从权限、身份、通信、可观测性和持久性五个方面分析其信任与控制机制；借助具有飞行代表性的NOS3模拟器，设计并实施五项实验，通过受损机载组件滥用合法架构权限来验证风险；同时与其他模块化飞行软件框架进行对比，归纳重复出现的信任假设和架构缺陷。

Result: 实验表明，单个受损组件即可利用系统中广泛共享的权限执行影响范围较大的操作，且相关行为难以与正常合法行为区分。跨框架比较进一步显示，组件间过度信任、权限共享和有限的内部隔离具有普遍性。

Conclusion: 现有飞行软件架构需要强化内部信任边界，重点包括细粒度权限控制、组件身份隔离、受保护的通信机制、增强的运行时可观测性以及持久化状态保护，从而降低单一组件受损后的横向影响和检测难度。

Abstract: As spacecraft become more software-driven and interconnected, onboard flight software is an increasingly important security boundary. Popular flight software architectures often treat onboard components as trusted peers, simplifying integration while limiting internal isolation and access control. We analyze NASA's Core Flight Software (cFS) to examine how authority, identity, communication, observability, and persistence are distributed across onboard components. Using NASA's flight-representative NOS3 simulator, we validate these weaknesses through five experiments implemented with a malicious onboard component that abuses legitimate architectural privileges. We then compare cFS with other modular flight software frameworks to identify recurring trust assumptions and architectural weaknesses. Our results show that a single compromised component can exploit broadly shared authority in ways that are difficult to distinguish from legitimate behavior. We conclude with architectural implications and discuss mechanisms for strengthening internal trust boundaries in future flight software systems.

</details>


### [41] [Finding Vulnerabilities via LLM-Augmented Semantics-Aware Type-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14533)
*Ruizhe Wang,Meng Xu,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SETYPE语义感知类型系统，利用大语言模型从源代码中理解符号和表达式的自然语言语义，进行类型推断与检查，以发现潜在软件漏洞。其Python原型PYSETYPE在真实Web应用上达到87%的检测精度和88%的检测准确率，并发现15个潜在零日漏洞，其中9个获开发者确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态分析主要依赖专家编写的语法规则，难以捕捉变量名、函数名及代码表达式中的深层语义。随着软件复杂度提升，仅依赖语法模式进行漏洞建模和检测的维护成本与局限性日益突出。

Method: 提出SETYPE语义感知类型系统，仅依据源代码中符号和表达式的自然语言含义构建类型信息。系统使用大语言模型执行类型推断和类型检查；当代码未通过类型检查时，将其标记为潜在漏洞。作者进一步实现面向Python Web应用的原型PYSETYPE。

Result: 在真实应用评测中，PYSETYPE取得87%的检测精度和88%的检测准确率。此外，系统识别出15个潜在零日漏洞，其中9个经开发者确认。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合大语言模型的代码语义理解能够增强静态漏洞检测能力，弥补传统语法规则在语义建模方面的不足。SETYPE及其PYSETYPE原型展现了语义感知类型检查用于实际漏洞发现的可行性，但其效果仍需在更广泛的语言、项目和漏洞类型上进一步验证。

Abstract: Vulnerability detection via static analysis traditionally relies on security experts encoding insecure coding patterns into algorithmic rules. However, this approach often focuses on syntactic patterns and overlooks deeper semantic information in the code, such as the meanings of variable and function names. As software systems grow more complex, modeling vulnerabilities using only syntactic rules becomes increasingly challenging.
  In this paper, we propose a semantics-aware approach to detecting software vulnerabilities. We present SETYPE, a semantics-aware type system that can be derived directly from source code based solely on the meanings of symbols and expressions in natural language. In the SETYPE type system, both type inference and checking are performed by Large Language Models (LLMs), and a failed type check indicates a potential vulnerability.
  We prototype PYSETYPE to demonstrate the feasibility of SETYPE for detecting vulnerabilities in Python web applications. Our evaluation on real-world applications achieves 87% detection precision and 88% detection accuracy. Using PYSETYPE, we identified 15 potential zero-day vulnerabilities, nine of which were confirmed by developers.

</details>

### [42] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RubricForge从少量带真实环境奖励的轨迹中反思式进化出可解释的文本评分标准，用于无环境访问的语言模型代理评估；其主要优势是降低对失败轨迹的误判，而非显著提升总体一致率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时环境奖励可能成本高、速度慢或不可用，因此需要可信的语言模型自动裁判。现有方法依赖手写评分标准或微调裁判模型，容易因轨迹表达流畅而错误地判定任务成功。

Method: RubricForge利用少量带真实结果标签的代理轨迹，通过反思式演化优化文本化评分标准，使其最大化与环境奖励的一致性；优化后冻结该标准，并在测试轨迹上通过单次模型调用完成评估，无需访问环境。评分标准以人类可读文本呈现，并明确列出判定依据。实验使用同一个冻结的7B模型作为代理和裁判，在tau-bench的173条标注轨迹和WebShop的160条数据上进行评估，并与通用G-Eval裁判比较。

Result: RubricForge与G-Eval的总体一致率差异不显著（McNemar检验p=0.248），绝对分数校准略逊于通用裁判（误差差异为-0.048，p=2×10^-4）。但在tau-bench上，RubricForge将失败轨迹误判为成功的比例从0.173降至0.115，且出现3次纠正、无反向错误；在具有等级结果的WebShop上，其排序相关性更高（Spearman相关系数0.410对比0.370）。

Conclusion: RubricForge的核心价值在于提升评估忠实度，尤其减少会导致不合格代理被部署的错误通过，而不是提升总体准确率或分数校准。对于无环境奖励的评估器，失败轨迹的误通过率比总体一致率更具有实际部署意义；同时，文本化标准增强了判定的可解释性和可追溯性。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [43] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析Qwen3.6-35B-A3B的MoE层级敏感性，发现专家屏蔽的影响高度依赖网络深度：早期和中间层较脆弱，后期尤其是第35–39层对低幅值专家屏蔽更具容忍度。基于此，后期定向屏蔽显著优于全层统一屏蔽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE通过稀疏激活扩展大语言模型，但不同层和专家对模型质量的相对重要性尚未得到充分刻画，限制了模型压缩与权重裁剪策略的设计。

Method: 在包含40个MoE层、每层256个专家、top-8路由的Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上，使用基于权重幅值的专家屏蔽方法，开展100、300和500条提示规模的多阶段实验。评估任务为XLCoST跨语言代码翻译，并比较全层屏蔽、不同深度区间屏蔽及top-k路由宽度从8降至6的效果。

Result: 层敏感性呈明显深度依赖性：第0–9层和第10–29层对专家屏蔽较敏感，第30–39层尤其第35–39层更能承受大幅屏蔽。在300条提示上，全层30%屏蔽仅保留150/300个Good或Similar结果，而后期定向策略保留249–255/300个结果，并屏蔽640–1,145个专家。在500条提示的验证集上，第35–39层屏蔽50%的策略取得最佳质量—压缩权衡，保留419/500个Good或Similar结果，仅屏蔽640个专家。将每个token的活跃专家数由8降至6在100条提示测试中显著降低实际运行时间，且未损失Good或Similar结果，但与激进专家屏蔽的组合尚不稳定。

Conclusion: MoE专家压缩不宜采用简单的全层统一屏蔽，深度感知的后期层定向策略更有效。研究结果为基于层敏感性的专家裁剪提供了实证依据，并支持后续开展物理权重删减、基于激活的专家重要性评估以及训练恢复研究。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [44] [Implementing Computational Law in Wolfram Language for the Governance of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13958)
*James K. Wiles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出以可执行的形式化法律规则治理无法完全解释推理过程的AI系统，并通过Wolfram Language实现Reified Input/Output Logic，评估GPT-4的法律文本形式化能力及其在具身智能体中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱式AI的治理不必依赖理解其内部推理，而应明确系统的义务、许可与禁止行为，并验证其是否合规。为实现可审计、可执行的治理，需要将法律规范转化为形式化规则。

Method: 在Wolfram Language中实现Reified Input/Output Logic及DAPRECO知识库相关形式，包括核心I/O公理、义务、许可、构成性规范、再化事件和时间算子；随后测试GPT-4将英文法律陈述转换为该形式化语言的能力，并通过一个依据计算合同运行的AI具身智能体案例进行验证。

Result: GPT-4存在多类转换错误，包括虚构函数、遗漏时间范围、偏离既定形式体系，以及生成能够运行且表面合理但实际编码了错误规范的程序。案例研究表明，形式化规则可以从合同直接延伸至智能体运行代码，并生成符号化、可审计的行为理由。

Conclusion: 计算法能够成为AI治理工具。理想目标是将那些能够且应当被程序执行的法律规范形式化，从而实现对AI行为的明确约束、自动检查与审计。

Abstract: How do we govern AI systems whose reasoning we cannot fully inspect? Governance does not require understanding a system's reasoning. It requires stating what the system is obliged, permitted, and forbidden to do, and checking whether it complied. I present an implementation of Reified Input/Output Logic, the formalism behind the DAPRECO knowledge base, in Wolfram Language: the core I/O axioms, obligations, permissions, constitutive norms, reified eventualities, and temporal operators. I then test whether GPT-4 can translate English legal statements into the formalism, and report the failures: hallucinated functions, omitted temporal scope, deviation from the formalism, and (in the worst cases) code that runs, reads plausibly, but silently encodes the wrong norm. A case study, an AI guard dog operating under a computational contract, shows how formalized rules can extend from a contract directly into the operational code of an embodied agent, producing symbolic, auditable justifications for its behaviour. I argue that computational law can be used as a governance tool and that a desirable goal would be to formalize the law that can and ought to be programmatically executable.

</details>


### [45] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，大语言模型在语言、形式推理、社会推理和物理推理等任务上形成了与人脑功能网络相似的模块化组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨人脑的功能模块化究竟是生物进化的偶然结果，还是智能系统普遍形成的一项基本原则。

Method: 研究者对大语言模型开展电路分析，覆盖四个认知领域、共46项任务，并比较不同任务所激活的神经元重叠模式与人脑功能网络的对应关系。

Result: 在人脑中依赖同一功能网络的任务，会在大语言模型中激活较为重叠的神经元；依赖不同功能网络的任务，则主要激活不同神经元，显示出跨任务的模块化组织。

Conclusion: 模块化架构能够在生物大脑和通过不同优化过程训练的大语言模型中趋同出现，说明模块化可能是智能系统组织和实现多种认知能力的基本属性。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>
